<p> The Vancouver Sun - RSS Feedhttps : **27;42922;TOOLONG , 06 Mar 2020 02:08:48 +0000Vancouver 's Grimes finally confirms Elon Musk is baby 's fatherhttp : **34;42951;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;42987;TOOLONG ... title= " Grimes . Promo shot for Miss Anthropocene release . " alt= " Grimes . Promo shot for Miss Anthropocene release . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43023;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1219584 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver musician Grimes has finally confirmed that Elon Musk is the father of her child . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s little surprise , really . Any other name would have been a complete shock to everyone , Musk included . But &lt;a href= " https : **33;43053;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;we did n't know for sure&lt;/a&gt; until the indie pop star , born Claire Boucher , made it official Thursday , in a &lt;a href= " https : **33;43088;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;digital cover story for Rolling Stone&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was probably a shrewd move for the deeply weird artist , now 26 weeks pregnant , to withhold that tidbit until now . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Boucher first revealed her pregnancy last month on Instagram . The surreal baby bump photo included a hyper-realistic rendering of a fetus in her belly and little clarifying information , raising several questions , such as : Is Grimes translucent ? And : Is this real ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; ( It&amp;rsquo ; s hard to be certain with Grimes , who once shared a truly absurd wellness routine that included screaming sessions and experimental e &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;ye surgery. ) &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The baby picture got people talking , and just in time for her fifth album . Miss Anthropocene was released to critical acclaim on Feb. 21. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43123;TOOLONG ... title= " Elon Musk and Grimes arrive for the 2018 Met Gala on May 7 , 2018 , at the Metropolitan Museum of Art in New York . " alt= " Elon Musk and Grimes arrive for the 2018 Met Gala on May 7 , 2018 , at the Metropolitan Museum of Art in New York . " data-portal-copyright= " ANGELA WEISS/AFP/Getty Images " **28;43159;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1185070 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Album opener So Heavy I Fell Through the Earth is about getting pregnant , Grimes told Rolling Stone . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was really profound to me when I decided I was going to do it , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; ( &amp;hellip ; ) I&amp;rsquo ; m just like , I have sacrificed my power in this moment . I have , like , capitulated , And I have spent my whole life avoiding that situation . I have never capitulated to anything , so it was just a profound commitment . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What inspired her to go through with it ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I do actually just really love my boyfriend , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; So I was like , &amp;lsquo ; You know , sure . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Baby Grimes , whose given name will very likely be a bizarre tech neologism or hyperbrainy pun , is due in early May . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;43189;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **35;43219;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **28;43256;TOOLONG " class= " twitter-follow-button " data-show-count= " false " &gt;Follow **25;43286;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;43313;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? NewsMusicEric Nam presents a new style of K-Pop with Before We Beginhttp : **35;43456;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;43493;TOOLONG ... title= " Eric Nam plays Vogue Theatre on March 6. " alt= " Eric Nam plays Vogue Theatre on March 6. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43529;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1226636 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Eric Nam&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Before We Begin world tour&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;43559;TOOLONG : March 6 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;43594;TOOLONG : Vogue Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : From $45 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;43630;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;43665;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Congratulations is not standard K-Pop . The single from Eric Nam&amp;rsquo ; s Before We Begin featuring American pop singer Marc E. Bassy boasts all the meticulous hooks and slinky production fans of the genre demand . But its chorus is anything but the usual soft-core romance resonating in global hits such as BTS&amp;rsquo ; Save Me , GFriend&amp;rsquo ; s Novillera , or vintage classics such as PSY&amp;rsquo ; s Gangnam Style . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **32;43694;TOOLONG finally leaving/Let 's throw a party here tonight/And toast to the end of you and I &lt;/em&gt; is more of a kiss-off than a kiss me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before We Begin is Nam&amp;rsquo ; s first all-English album , and the Korean-American singer &amp;mdash ; who was named GQ Korea&amp;rsquo ; s 2016 man of the year and flagged by Forbes as one of the 30 Under 30 Asia 2017 &amp;mdash ; wanted to dish up something slightly different than the chart-topping , R&amp;amp ; B-tinged tunes on EPs such as Cloud 9 and Honestly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While some of the new album&amp;rsquo ; s tunes are English-language reworking of past hits , Nam wanted to offer up something different to potential new fans . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nam has long been made in South Korea and across much of Asia , performing at major festivals . He also is a regular presence on TV , hosting programs such as Ariang TV&amp;rsquo ; s After School Club , the ASC After Show , and even hosted a pop-up restaurant program devoted to cuisine cooked by an international group of grandmas. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Postmedia chatted with the Atlanta-born and raised celebrity : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Given your Atlanta roots and Boston College education in business , how did you wind up a star in Korea ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;43728;TOOLONG : It was a combination of a lot of luck , ( and ) a lot of hard work that lead me to this . I had accepted a position as a business analyst at Deloitte Consulting in New York . But before I went into that workforce , I decided to take a year off and went to India to do a social enterprise fellowship . It wasn&amp;rsquo ; t the best fit , but that was where a TV show in Korea found me and invited me to first come perform . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Was it an instant fit for you being in the K-Pop scene ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;43760;TOOLONG : Not at all . Trying to establish my base took a few years . I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know the culture well , or how to fit in , and must have met with at least 15 labels before finding something that worked . Record deals there can be for singles , or they can be for a certain time period . I put in a stipulation that if my album wasn&amp;rsquo ; t out in a year , I could leave to go back to Deloitte . I signed August of 2012 , and by January 2013 I had my first album out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : It seems like many artists are successfully moving out of Korea and into the global market . And they do it without English , too . What made you decide to make the new album in English ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;43792;TOOLONG : When I first toured North America , everyone was saying that it was working because there is such as a solid Korean fan base here . The truth is , that is less than five per cent of the audience , which is mixed of all backgrounds . I think K-Pop is something that sucks people in because it&amp;rsquo ; s open . I can do pop , EDM , rock , R&amp;amp ; B and it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t matter , K-Pop embraces them all . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Plus , you crank the performance aspect up a lot do n't you ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;43824;TOOLONG : There was a point when dancing to music became cheesy after the boy band era of NSync and Backstreet Boys , but there were always those who craved that kind of visual satisfaction . K-Pop really filled that void , because it&amp;rsquo ; s so geared to spectacle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Do you think you have paved the way to build a global career from your K-Pop position ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; It hasn&amp;rsquo ; t really happened unless you are talking BTS &amp;mdash ; and they can do anything they want to because they are so big . For a solo singer/songwriter like me , it&amp;rsquo ; s new . But I have done a lot of collaborating with acts like Solange , Timbaland and others and made a point to mix English into my Korean songs . Yes , the new album was a risk , but I enjoy a challenge and pushing boundaries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;43856;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;43891;TOOLONG &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;43927;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;43969;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;44005;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;44036;TOOLONG ... latest music **25;44072;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;44099;TOOLONG " **36;44130;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;44168;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;44197;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynMon , 02 Mar 2020 22:08:43 **45;44228;TOOLONG ... Suzuki puts himself under microscope for new show on aginghttp : **35;44275;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;44312;TOOLONG ... title= " David Suzuki undergoes an eye exam , one of the many tests he put his 83-year-old self through for a new The Nature of Things episode on aging . " alt= " David Suzuki undergoes an eye exam , one of the many tests he put his 83-year-old self through for a new The Nature of Things episode on aging . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of CBC " **28;44348;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1217817 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; When his boss at the CBC first brought up the idea of aging as a topic for &lt;a href= " https : **33;44378;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Nature of Things&lt;/a&gt; , host &lt;a href= " https : **33;44413;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;David Suzuki&lt;/a&gt; was interested but not sold on the concept . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The plan presented to the scientist and environmentalist was about how science was going to make us all live way , way longer than the average 84 years for women and 80 years for men . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I looked at the proposal . And it was a typical thing . You know , science is discovering ways of getting fruit flies to live 50 per cent longer , and they&amp;rsquo ; re doing all of these fancy DNA things , and maybe scientists are gon na extend life by 70 or 80 years . And I said , &amp;lsquo ; This is such bull&amp;mdash ; t , &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; the 83-year-old Suzuki , co-founder of the &lt;a href= " https : //davidsuzuki.org/about/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;David Suzuki Foundation&lt;/a&gt; , said over the phone from his Vancouver office . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Suzuki explained that humans have evolved to live a certain amount of time and we&amp;rsquo ; ve pushed that mark a bit through science . In the end , though , for him , it comes down to quality not quantity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re living longer , but you know , after the age of 65 or 70 , things are not that great , &amp;rdquo ; added Suzuki . &amp;ldquo ; Who wants another 60 years of a sh&amp;mdash ; y life ? I am much more interested in how do we age well . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So that&amp;rsquo ; s what the new Nature of Things&amp;rsquo ; episode Aging Well Suzuki Style is all about . The show , which is one of the handful of episodes Suzuki hosts each season , airs on Feb. 28 on CBC-TV at 9 p.m. and streams on CBC Gem . The show offers a fascinating and very personal take on getting old . And it&amp;rsquo ; s not surprising that Suzuki , for the most part , does well on the tests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve got the tears of a 20-year-old , &amp;rdquo ; the famed scientist and environmentalist said with a laugh . Another key strength for Suzuki was he had pretty good balance , a major key to aging well and avoiding injury . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44448;TOOLONG ... title= " When it comes to aging well , balance is a key component in keeping seniors going . Here David Suzuki goes through the motions of testing his own balance during a new The Nature of Things episode on aging that hits CBC-TV and CBC Gem on Feb. 28 . Photo : CBC " alt= " When it comes to aging well , balance is a key component in keeping seniors going . Here David Suzuki goes through the motions of testing his own balance during a new The Nature of Things episode on aging that hits CBC-TV and CBC Gem on Feb. 28 . Photo : CBC " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of CBC " **28;44484;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1217820 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; He credits an early physical lifestyle of farm and construction work as a basis for his strong senior shape . He still rides his bike regularly , works out and walks whenever he can . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While his early life consisted of physical work , all that went out the window when he turned to a life of science . It took a bit of insecurity to motivate him to take better care of himself . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When I got into the lab , I was sitting , looking through a microscope for hours and I developed a pot ( belly ) when I married Tara ( Cullis ) . I was quite chubby , &amp;rdquo ; said Suzuki , who admits his younger wife made him take stock . &amp;ldquo ; Here I am , this guy with a big pot . S&amp;ndash ; t , I better get into some kind of shape . I mean she&amp;rsquo ; s a lot younger than me . So , I started working out . And that was , to me , really lucky . I was doing it for the wrong reasons . I&amp;rsquo ; m doing it to try to stay up to my wife , but I was doing it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Suzuki points to the well-worn mantra of exercise being a key to a healthy , longer life . But he also quickly points out that humans seem to be fighting that basic idea at every turn . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The way we&amp;rsquo ; ve constructed society is to go to the opposite end everything is for convenience . Right ? So rather than walking up a flight of stairs , there is an escalator . Or , you know , the car services , &amp;rdquo ; said Suzuki . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Maintaining a healthy diet and healthy relationships also help in the quality-of-life department . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; People have to have motivation . They have to have a place in society or something they do with other people , whether it&amp;rsquo ; s going to karate lessons or , you know , those sorts of things that give you a sense that you still have value in society , &amp;rdquo ; said Suzuki . &amp;ldquo ; For me , it&amp;rsquo ; s being a grandfather . This is the most joyful and &amp;hellip ; most important time in my life because I&amp;rsquo ; ve got these grandchildren ( he has nine in total , three in their 20s and six younger ones ) . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Your later years will also be improved by doing something meaningful , something that could be of value to the rest of society , he says . For Suzuki , that social currency comes in the form of activism , writing books , speeches and , of course , his TV gig . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Well , you know , the Nature of Things , I think that is a huge part of keeping me engaged because I&amp;rsquo ; m very proud of this series , &amp;rdquo ; said Suzuki , who has become a household name over the course of his four decades of TV science . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m very proud to be part of it . And , you know , it&amp;rsquo ; s a joy . It keeps me learning . Every show is a learning experience for me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But , you know , I&amp;rsquo ; m not immortal . I&amp;rsquo ; m going to kick the bucket &amp;mdash ; and I hope the show goes on . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And he hopes it goes on with his daughters Severn and Sarika Cullis-Suzuki . For the record , Sarika has already co-hosted a few shows with her dad and hosted a couple of her own Nature of Things as well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44514;TOOLONG ... title= " Nature of Things TV host and environmentalist David Suzuki with daughter , Sarika Cullis-Suzuki . Photo : Ian Smith/Vancouver Sun " alt= " Nature of Things TV host and environmentalist David Suzuki with daughter , Sarika Cullis-Suzuki . Photo : Ian Smith/Vancouver Sun " data-portal-copyright= " Ian Smith " **28;44550;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1204321 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the show and his family keeps him motivated to stay healthy , he admits that it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to stay happy as he watches more pipelines getting green-lit and trolls attacking a teen activist . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , this foreboding sense is not new for Suzuki . Sarika , in an earlier interview with Postmedia News , said her dad was often Dr. Doom and Gloom . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When told about that moniker he laughed , but then turned serious . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Periodically I go through depression , despair , &amp;rdquo ; said Suzuki . &amp;ldquo ; Fortunately , and my wife is the same , we don&amp;rsquo ; t go into despair at the same time , so we can pull each other out of that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the end , though , he says those feelings subside , for a bit , and he is reminded &amp;mdash ; and likes to remind others &amp;mdash ; that giving up isn&amp;rsquo ; t an option and that , by joining forces , change can happen . He sticks a pin in that point with this story : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Do you know the famous story of the hummingbird and the forest fire ? It&amp;rsquo ; s an Indigenous story . A hummingbird is flying through the woods and suddenly sees this huge forest fire . So , he whips over to a lake , picks up a beak full of water . It goes back and drops it on the fire , and he&amp;rsquo ; s whizzing back and forth this way , and all the other animals in the forest are laughing at him and saying : &amp;lsquo ; What the hell are you doing ? &amp;rsquo ; And the hummingbird looks at them and says : &amp;lsquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m doing the best I can . &amp;rsquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re all those hummingbirds . If there are enough hummingbirds , damn it , we can put the fire out . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A good way to become a hummingbird is to just take time to be in and part of the natural world &amp;mdash ; a concept Suzuki has long since championed in his bid to get people interested in protecting our tenuous ecosystems . He says he is always surprised by how little people pay attention to the world around them . Whether they&amp;rsquo ; re riding a bus or running . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was with my grandson Ryo . We were on a path in Victoria and I spot an owl . It was a huge owl . Maybe people are used to seeing owls but I haven&amp;rsquo ; t seen many wild owls . I run over with the buggy and I&amp;rsquo ; m , &amp;lsquo ; Look , Ryo , look . &amp;rsquo ; I am taking pictures and not one person stopped to look where I was looking or said , &amp;lsquo ; What are you doing ? , &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; said Suzuki . &amp;ldquo ; So they are all doing their thing running and biking , but they&amp;rsquo ; re not interested in what&amp;rsquo ; s going on . And , you know , the brain is a novelty-seeking device . That bird is a really exciting part of my environment , but people are shut down . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;lsquo ; Shut down&amp;rsquo ; they must be . After all , who wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t want to stop when they see one of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s most recognizable figures standing on a path excitedly pointing at a tree ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Walking outside people don&amp;rsquo ; t notice who I am , &amp;rdquo ; said Suzuki . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s just an old man with a kid . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No , not really . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;44580;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;44607;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;44641;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; Dana GeeThu , 27 Feb 2020 18:11:17 **45;44672;TOOLONG ... came to Reinhart via Langley&amp;rsquo ; s Furever Freed Dog Rescue Society . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The non-profit was started in 2013 by Lisa Gaye and has rescued more than 3,500 dogs from across I 've always stood behind adopting dogs and I 'm so beyond thrilled to have found @thelabellefoundation who do incredible work fostering little abandoned babies and finding them homes like this baby girl ? ? ? ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style= " color : #c9c8cd ; font-family:Arial , sans-serif ; font-size:14px ; line-height:17px ; margin-bottom:0 ; margin-top:8px ; overflow:hidden ; padding:8px 0 7px ; text-align:center ; text-overflow:ellipsis ; white-space:nowrap ; " &gt;A post shared by &lt;a href= " https : **29;46239;TOOLONG ? utm ... style= " color : #c9c8cd ; font-family:Arial , sans-serif ; font-size:14px ; font-style:normal ; font-weight:normal ; line-height:17px ; " target= " blank " &gt; Madelaine Petsch&lt;/a&gt; ( @madelame ) on &lt;time style= " font-family:Arial , sans-serif ; font-size:14px ; line-height:17px ; " datetime= " **25;46270;TOOLONG " &gt;Oct 26 , 2019 at 2:46pm **45;46297;TOOLONG ... &lt;script async src= " **28;46344;TOOLONG " &gt;&lt;/sc ... &lt;p&gt; Riverdale premiered in 2017 and is currently airing its fourth season . The show films in and around Metro Vancouver , with its studio and several high-profile filming locations in Langley . &lt;/p&gt; Stephanie IpThu , 27 Feb 2020 14:22:11 **45;46374;TOOLONG ... NewsTelevisionAlma Deutscher dishes on writing operas , ballroom dancing in Vienna and playing with the VSOhttp : **37;46421;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;46460;TOOLONG ... title= " Child prodigy pianist/violinist and composer Alma Deutscher will perform five shows with the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra . She is just 15. " alt= " Child prodigy pianist/violinist and composer Alma Deutscher will perform five shows with the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra . She is just 15. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;46496;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1207185 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Alma Deutscher with the VSO&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;46526;TOOLONG : Feb. 28 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;46561;TOOLONG : Bell Performing Arts Centre , 6250 144 St. , Surrey &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;46597;TOOLONG : Feb. 29 , 30 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;46632;TOOLONG : Orpheum Theatre , 601 Smithe , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;46668;TOOLONG : March 1 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;46703;TOOLONG : Centennial Theatre , 2300 Lonsdale , North Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and Info&lt;/strong&gt; : &lt;a href= " https : **33;46739;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;46774;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Composer Alma Deutscher premiered her Piano Concerto in E-flat major at the Carinthian Summer Music Festival in Austria in 2017 . She also performed her Violin Concerto No. 1 in G at the same event . Only a year earlier , conductor Zubin Mehta premiered her full-length opera , Cinderella , in Vienna . That&amp;rsquo ; s quite an impressive list of achievement for any classical musician . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Deutscher was 12. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Born in Basingstoke , England , in 2005 , the musical prodigy composed her first piano sonata at age six . At seven , she had penned a short opera based upon author Neil Gaiman&amp;rsquo ; s short story the Sweeper of dreams . Three years later , she produced Cinderella . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By that time , the world had taken notice of the confident young artist&amp;rsquo ; s prodigious talents , with many in the press dubbing her &amp;ldquo ; the next Mozart . &amp;rdquo ; Unwilling to wear that title , and noting that there already was a Mozart , the artist has gone on record stating she would prefer to be recognized for who she is : Alma. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Vienna is an incredibly inspiring place to grow up , it is really the city of music , &amp;rdquo ; said Deutscher , over the phone from her home in Austria . &amp;ldquo ; When I go to the vineyards with the amazing view of the city to skip around in the nature , or when I&amp;rsquo ; m sitting in the underground , I&amp;rsquo ; m constantly getting ideas . Also , having lived here awhile , I&amp;rsquo ; ve also composed a few Viennese waltzes. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; These waltzes will almost certainly be imbued with the light and feathery touch that characterize the era when the House of Habsburg ruled the region . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Critics writing about Deutscher&amp;rsquo ; s work have lit upon how it is very focused on melody , running counter to many of the atonal and arrhythmic facets of contemporary classical music . This somewhat tiresome argument bred in an academic circles that contemporary music must be ugly and dissonant to reflect these qualities in the world doesn&amp;rsquo ; t impress Alma. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her lush , symphonic music defies the critics . They certainly can&amp;rsquo ; t accuse her of failing to find inspiration in those same mechanics and technology of everyday life . Her recently composed Siren Sounds Waltz is informed by exactly what modernists say music should embrace . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It just happens to be melodic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Viennese police siren is made in fourths&amp;nbsp ; and doesn&amp;rsquo ; t wail like most do in other places , so I decided to make it into a melody , &amp;rdquo ; Deutscher said . &amp;ldquo ; Then I also incorporated specific sounds such as local bus doors closing and other city noise and integrated them into a waltz . It begins very chaotically , with the orchestra playing like a street with horns and sirens , until it calms down and you enter into the imagination . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;46806;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;46842;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; That imaginary realm is where the &amp;ldquo ; ugly sounds of the modern world become something that is beautiful . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Deutscher&amp;rsquo ; s imaginary realm is inseparable from her musical output . She has conceived a land she calls Transylvanian &amp;mdash ; never having been to Transylvania , she says she has no idea how that name entered her head &amp;mdash ; which is where she hears beautiful melodies that wind-up in finished works . These are often written by various uniquely-named composers in the land , such as Antonin Yellowsink. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; All the composers in my imaginary country write a little bit differently , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Antonin is quite a melancholy character who writes sad melodies . Sometimes , when I&amp;rsquo ; m stuck in my compositions and don&amp;rsquo ; t know what to do , I show them to him and he often makes very good suggestions . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The strong female characters in her operas undergo adventures in Transylvanian too . Deutscher has always loved stories about girls who overcame difficulty through their talents and their determination . Due to this , she populates her works with them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s what I like , and it is also is a way for me to address how , in the past , there were talented female musicians who were denied the right to compose and have their works performed , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; That really made me rather quite angry . When I write a piece , working hard to develop the melodies into a something that makes senses , with a structure that makes sense ; I think of them . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The more the interview continues , the more it becomes clear why Alma has enchanted everyone from Zubin Mehta to some of the classical music world&amp;rsquo ; s toughest critics and beloved British presenter Stephen Fry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her love of music is absolute , and completely impossible to separate from any other facet of her life . This artist was finding ways to note down the melodies that apparently come into her head constantly when she was six years-old and had no formal musical training to speak of . Her ongoing studying has , in large part , been about developing better techniques to transition what she hears to sheet music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Deutscher went on the record at age 10 stating that she understood how it might be hard for some to take her music seriously because of her years . The 15 year-old on the other end of the line exudes a professional confidence coupled with youthful energy that begs the question : How far can it go ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m always composing , but it&amp;rsquo ; s a very hard and time-consuming process , taking those melodies that are constantly coming and organizing them , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; And for pieces such as the piano concerto I&amp;rsquo ; m doing in Vancouver , which I wrote when I was 12 , I am always doing little fixes and changes . I re-orchestrated almost the whole concerti to put in a big brass section , because I&amp;rsquo ; ve always wanted it to have a big crashing section that&amp;rsquo ; s really loud , and never felt that it worked quite right . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She says that the piano concerto holds a particular place in her heart , because she wrote a good deal of it on her grandmother&amp;rsquo ; s piano after she passed on . She says that it&amp;rsquo ; s one of the saddest things she has written , surprising to people who know what an incredibly happy person she is. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So what does she do besides music ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I love ballroom dancing , and Vienna is a perfect place for that , and it&amp;rsquo ; s become a serious passion , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Twice a week , I get to go and learn the most amazing dances , and I&amp;rsquo ; m going to go to lots of balls this coming season with my dresses already picked out . And I also love the theatre . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alma Deutscher will play three performances with the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra under composer Stanley Dodds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " sderdeyn@postmedia.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;46878;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;46916;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;46945;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynFri , 21 Feb 2020 19:00:30 **45;46976;TOOLONG ... " alt= " Alexandra Mauler-Steinmann and Barbara Klebanoff co-chaired the 30th-annual Vancouver Symphony Ball that benefited the VSO and its 17-year-old school of music that is unique for Canadian orchestras . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry/ " **28;47096;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1214192 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; ORCHESTRAL VARIATIONS : Third-time co-chair Alexandra Mauler-Steinmann and newcomer Barbara Klebanoff fronted the 30th annual Vancouver Symphony Ball that reportedly raised $790,000 and change recently . Good news for recently appointed VSO board chair Etienne Bruson . Ditto for Angela Elster who was three weeks into her job as president-CEO of the orchestra and its unique-in-Canada music school . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47126;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver Symphony Orchestra maestro Otto Tausk and new president-CEO Angela Elster made their debut appearances at the Symphony Ball . " alt= " Vancouver Symphony Orchestra maestro Otto Tausk and new president-CEO Angela Elster made their debut appearances at the Symphony Ball . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry/ " **28;47162;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1214195 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As usual , the gala offered something no other can : Maestro Otto Tausk conducting the VSO through Bizet&amp;rsquo ; s Carmen Suite No. 1 and de Falla&amp;rsquo ; s Siete canciones populares espa&amp;ntilde ; olas . William Rowson then took the baton for the event&amp;rsquo ; s ever-favourite Lehar and Strauss waltzes along with foxtrots , rumbas , tangos and swing-era tunes . The gala was themed to complement a concert by the orchestra and Canadian mezzo-soprano Rihab Chaieb titled Carmen : Sounds of Spain . That induced many attendees to wear red , black and lace while tucking into Spanish appetizers and less-Hispanic ( and hardly kosher ) ham-wrapped ling cod . Two chaps sported kilts , while **26;47192;TOOLONG Rail chair David McLean wore the tartan pants that Scots call trews . Such garb intrigued Utrecht-born Tausk . Perhaps he&amp;rsquo ; ll adopt it for an Orpheum theatre workout with Mendelssohn&amp;rsquo ; s Hebrides Overture. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47220;TOOLONG ... title= " Crazy 8s directors ( front ) Brodi-Jo Scalise , Josh Aries , Daniel Irving and ( rear ) Ali Liebert , Bruna Arbex and Malibu Taetz received cash to shoot their movies in one week . Photo credit : Ken Phillips Publicity Group " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47830;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206757 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;JFL Comedy Festival&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 13-24 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **28;47860;TOOLONG ? sear ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;47890;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the most popular shows on TV is Fox&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " https : **30;47922;TOOLONG ? ci ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Masked Singer&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The show is kind of a fever dream mixed with a singing competition . Each week celebrities hide their identities under elaborate costumes ( this upcoming season will include among the 18 contestants a llama , banana and astronaut ) while they sing ( apparently ) their hearts out in a bid to woo the judges and of course America . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Comedian &lt;a href= " http : //margaretcho.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Margaret Cho&lt;/a&gt; , who will be in Vancouver for a &lt;a href= " http : //jflnorthwest.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;JFL Northwest&lt;/a&gt; comedy festival show ( Feb. 15 , 7 p.m. at the Vogue , tickets $39 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;47954;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;47989;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt; ) &lt;/em&gt; , was the Poodle in Season 1 of the show . The current popularity of the show that started in Korea is no surprise to Cho . She agrees the wacky talent show is a kind of antidote to the emotional and politically polarizing heaviness that many are feeling these days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s something innocent about it , which I think is something that is needed , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho over the phone from her home in Los Angeles recently . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think we do need something we can kinda come together on that&amp;rsquo ; s very playful . It&amp;rsquo ; s unique , and at the same time it&amp;rsquo ; s something very important because we need a childlike wonder about things . I think it&amp;rsquo ; s really sweet . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s also free of labels and unconscious biases , as a singing egg and pineapple are equally nutty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Identity is put aside and right now there is so much caustic friction that people have about identity in particular , and so when you remove that sort of aspect of it and just look at the purity of somebody&amp;rsquo ; s voice , I think there is a tenderness there , and a humanity , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48018;TOOLONG ... title= " Margaret Cho. " alt= " Margaret Cho. " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of JFL " **28;48054;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206765 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; And for the record : It is hard to sing in a mask . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When you&amp;rsquo ; re singing you&amp;rsquo ; re never enclosed in something , so it is really hard to tell how you are doing . Sonically , it is very different , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Cho can throw down as a glamorous singing poodle , do successful sitcoms , host a podcast , design clothes , and act as a BDSM expert on the Netflix show Explained , the Grammy- and Emmy-nominated comic always manages to find her way back to the stand-up stage &amp;mdash ; a place she has called home since she was 16. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cho is part of a JFL Feb. 13-25 line-up that includes comedy headliners Hannah Gadsby , Bill Burr and Patton Oswalt as well as up-and-coming stand-ups , improv and sketch performances , hit podcasts and even short films . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48084;TOOLONG ... title= " Red-hot comedian Hannah Gadsby will be bringing her new show Douglas to the JFL Northwest comedy festival Feb. 24 and 25 . Photo : Jill Greenberg " alt= " Red-hot comedian Hannah Gadsby will be bringing her new show Douglas to the JFL Northwest comedy festival Feb. 24 and 25 . Photo : Jill Greenberg " data-portal-copyright= " Jill Greenberg " **28;48120;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206763 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; JFL is just one of about 100 stand-up gigs Cho does each year . &amp;nbsp ; While shows like this one make up the base of her three-decade-long career , she says she also needs variety for complete happiness . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think they all kind of work together to make for a balanced life . I love acting . I love music , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; To me it is really special to do all these different things , but comedy is a constant . It&amp;rsquo ; s something that is always happening so that is the most important part of my life . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cho grew up in San Francisco where her parents ran a gay bookstore . A self-identifying bisexual , Cho has always been a vocal supporter of LBGTQ communities and , in turn , those communities have supported her work . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I love that . To me that&amp;rsquo ; s still a big part of my identity and kind of my purpose . So , yeah , I love that , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho about her fan base . &amp;ldquo ; I love being queer . I love the queerness of my existence . It&amp;rsquo ; s the essence of who I am. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s also a big part of what she wants to talk about , but it by no means is her only topic when she takes to the stand-up stage . Like many comedians who call America home , the current state of their union is loaded with potential material . And really , isn&amp;rsquo ; t it better to laugh than cry ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What&amp;rsquo ; s good is there is a lot to say and there is a lot to deal with in all of these issues , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There are things that I have talked about for a long time , whether it is racism , sexism , homophobia . All of these things have a big effect on our lives now , so there is a lot to say and that is what&amp;rsquo ; s great about comedy &amp;mdash ; that I can go out and do that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;48150;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;48192;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;48228;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;48259;TOOLONG ... latest Metro Vancouver arts **25;48295;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Obviously at the eye of the liberal comedy storm is U.S. President Donald Trump . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cho doesn&amp;rsquo ; t hesitate when asked about him . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Him getting elected , the whole thing has been upsetting from the minute it happened . I really didn&amp;rsquo ; t think it was possible , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It is such a sickening situation . He is acting like this office is kind of a plaything for him . It&amp;rsquo ; s a game . He&amp;rsquo ; s not even taking it seriously . It is really terrifying . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cho said she still finds MAGA hat wearers unnerving and disappointing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The blind faith people put in him ( Trump ) &amp;mdash ; they don&amp;rsquo ; t know he has no experience as a politician . They seem to look at this office as an extension of reality TV , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s kind of like high school , but it is way worse and it is real . They indulge him and his ego , but at the same time they put this faith in him that is really undeserved and it is really misplaced . That is really what&amp;rsquo ; s sad to me is that they have no idea what is going on . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It has to do with their own biases. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Cho has a lot to say about the current U.S. political landscape , she refuses to let the reality of partisan politics remove all hope from her world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think we got so far ahead &amp;hellip ; only to take several steps back , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think it is a process , and we have also made a lot of gains in the way we talk about the planet and the way we talk about climate change ; the way we talk about trying to close the gap of sexism ; the way we now talk about gay rights in a more nuanced and productive way . I feel like there is definitely progress . I feel like social media has enhanced our idea about equality and ways to get it . There&amp;rsquo ; s hope there among all this other stuff . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After all , a show like Masked Singer starring big , singing toys is a hit , so anything can happen , right ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Work is fun for this showbiz veteran . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I just really enjoy what I do , &amp;rdquo ; said Cho , who has worked in Vancouver lots , including recording with pop duo Tegan and Sara . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s lots of fun in it . I have a lot of pleasure doing it . It&amp;rsquo ; s a really treasured part of my life and I think that&amp;rsquo ; s part of it . I think it&amp;rsquo ; s a big part of it , the enthusiasm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s never about getting jaded or over it . I love it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;* As well as the 7 p.m. show on Feb. 15 , Cho will be at the Vancity Theatre at 5 p.m. for an In Conversation with Margaret Cho event . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;48322;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;48364;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Now 19 , Casey weighs 25 kg , is 122 cm tall , and the movie has become integral to a play based on his own words that was written and directed by Ben Ratner . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48810;TOOLONG ... title= " Long-time drama teacher Jim Crescenzo is seen with members of the East End Boys Club he founded and that presented the Casey and the Octopus play . The club was founded by Octopus co-executive producer Jim Crescenzo who taught two generations of Templeton theatre students . One grad thanked him &amp;ldquo ; for helping me stand on my own two feet , which are strong and no longer let me fall . &amp;rdquo ; Casey Wright&amp;rsquo ; s own feet can be shaky , courtesy of a 2013 stroke that paralyzed his right side and rendered speech impossible . Lengthy therapy restored the latter enough for him to advise audiences : &amp;ldquo ; When you&amp;rsquo ; re climbing that mountain and the clouds part to show you there&amp;rsquo ; s still a long way to go , tighten your boots , take a deep breath , and keep climbing . &amp;rdquo ; His father , Larry , says the &amp;ldquo ; Octopus&amp;rdquo ; in the play&amp;rsquo ; s title refers to what the first brain tumour looked like in medical scans . Casey subsequently refused scar-reducing surgery . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m keeping &amp;rsquo ; em , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; They tell the story of what I am and what I&amp;rsquo ; ve been through . &amp;rdquo ; That is a lot , with more perhaps to come . Nevertheless , he ends the play on his feet and telling audiences : &amp;ldquo ; If I had been &amp;lsquo ; normal&amp;rsquo ; I wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t be standing here talking to you guys . And talking to you is what makes me feel alive . It&amp;rsquo ; s what makes me get out of bed in the morning and keep going . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Despite his enormous medical travails , natural-showman Casey has had many more life experiences , including sky-diving and honorary RCMP staff-sergeant-major status , than many of his contemporaries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s a word for that : Outstanding . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48876;TOOLONG ... title= " Casey and the Octopus co-executive producer Danny Virtue says he 's negotiating with broadcasters for a revival of his locally shot TV series , Neon Rider . Terry&amp;rsquo ; s brother Jack is a world&amp;rsquo ; s-top-ten piper and the SFU band&amp;rsquo ; s pipe sergeant . His sons Andrew and John are pipers there , too . " alt= " Prince Harry and Meghan , the Duchess of Sussex , will split their time between Canada and the U.K. , the Queen announced on Monday after a family summit at Sandringham. " data-portal-copyright= " Michele Spatari/AFP via Getty Images " **28;50353;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1188371 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;50383;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meghan Markle and Prince Harry are a trendy transnational couple &amp;mdash ; and no more so than with their potential choice of B.C. as the chosen land in which to set up a provisional home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The British Duke of Sussex and his American bride are by no means the first foreign-born rich people to come to B.C. as non-permanent residents , determined to enjoy the temperate region&amp;rsquo ; s live-and-let-live ethos and its ethnically diverse people with a penchant for the outdoors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The &lt;a href= " https : **33;50419;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;province last fall become the fastest-growing region&lt;/a&gt; in all of Canada , a country which Statistics Canada says is itself numerically expanding at levels &lt;a href= " https : **33;50454;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; " the highest ever observed . " &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Like Meghan and Harry , more than four out of five new people in Canada and B.C. are not born in the country . They&amp;rsquo ; re from offshore . B.C. , with its high real-estate prices , is receiving an increasingly smaller fraction of migrants from other Canadian provinces . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another thing that makes Prince Harry and Meghan like the largest cohort of new arrivals is the couple appear to be preparing to become temporary residents . They&amp;rsquo ; re planning on settling in Canada to a certain degree , but they&amp;rsquo ; re giving no indications they&amp;rsquo ; re here to obtain citizenship . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even though the world has been watching as Meghan and son Archie have spent much of the past two months on Vancouver Island and in Metro Vancouver &amp;mdash ; and Queen Elizabeth has acknowledged the family is going &amp;ldquo ; through a period of transition in which the Duke and Duchess will spend time in Canada and the United Kingdom&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; several things are unknown . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;50489;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;50531;TOOLONG ... Todd : Five things we learned about migration in **25;50567;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;50594;TOOLONG ... housing market pressed by temporary **30;50630;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;50662;TOOLONG ... Todd : A tale of two costly housing markets : Toronto and **30;50698;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; The exact migration status of Meghan and Harry is up in the air , for instance . Still , the couple fit loosely into the category of &lt;a href= " http : **34;50730;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; " millionaire migrants . " &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s the title of the seminal book University of B.C. geographer David Ley wrote to describe the escalating movement of transnational wealthy people flocking to the world&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; gateway&amp;rdquo ; cities , such as Vancouver , in search of some sort of haven . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And should the moneyed royal couple buy a home in this country , they would be signing in on the current global fashion in real estate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canada and B.C. &amp;mdash ; especially high-priced Metro Vancouver , Victoria and &lt;a href= " http : **34;50766;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Toronto&lt;/a&gt; ( where Markle has worked as an actress ) &amp;mdash ; are prime destinations for the world&amp;rsquo ; s wealthy to purchase properties . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If Harry and Meghan put down money on a dwelling in Metro Vancouver , Victoria or in certain other urban centres on the West Coast , however , Vancouver immigration lawyer Sam Hyman says they would be subject to the B.C. government&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " http : **34;50802;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;foreign-buyers tax&lt;/a&gt; &amp;mdash ; and perhaps to its speculation and vacancy tax . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In that way , the famous couple would end up in the same boat as tens of thousands of other foreign national property owners in B.C. who do not spend more than six months a year in the country ( which normally means Ottawa does not tax their income ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The NDP government is already beginning to take in hundreds of millions of dollars a year from targeting lightly- used , non-resident-owned B.C. properties with such taxes , which are similar to those in many sought-after global cities , including London , home to Buckingham Palace . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since they are British and American citizens , Harry and Meghan are distinct in one way . They do not hail from the continent that is by far the largest source of new arrivals in Canada , Asia . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As such they do not have to apply for a visitor&amp;rsquo ; s visa to make a home in Canada for up to six months a year . That distinguishes them from the more than three million people from countries such as China and India who have in recent years been &lt;a href= " http : **34;50838;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;snapping up Canada 's 10-year-multiple-entry visas&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50874;TOOLONG ... title= " Queen Elizabeth II , Meghan , Duchess of Sussex , Prince Harry , Duke of Sussex at Buckingham Palace for an event to mark the centenary of the RAF , July 10 , 2018. " alt= " Queen Elizabeth II , Meghan , Duchess of Sussex , Prince Harry , Duke of Sussex at Buckingham Palace for an event to mark the centenary of the RAF , July 10 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Chris Jackson/Getty Images " **28;50910;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1189257 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yet long-term visitors such as Harry , Meghan and Archie add in a way to the country&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " http : **34;50940;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;exploding stock of non-permanent residents&lt;/a&gt; , a cohort made up mostly of international students and various types of guest workers . This group makes up two of three of the roughly one million new migrants to Canada each year , as well as of the more than 110,000 moving annually into B.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Regardless of the category of non-permanent resident to which t &lt;a href= " http : **34;50976;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;he royal family&lt;/a&gt; end up belonging , the consequences end up being similar in regards to lifestyle options &amp;mdash ; especially when it comes to Canada&amp;rsquo ; s housing market . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even though it&amp;rsquo ; s anybody&amp;rsquo ; s guess whether Prince Harry and Meghan will buy property in B.C. , it is common for non-permanent residents to do so . Such foreign nationals are free to come and go almost as they wish , whether to visit family or enjoy part-time living near evergreen forests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Without commenting directly on the royal couple , veteran &lt;a href= " http : **34;51012;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;B.C. real-estate analyst Steve Saretsky&lt;/a&gt; says the overall migration-fuelled growth in Canada&amp;rsquo ; s population that the arrival of the royal couple epitomizes has been &amp;ldquo ; nothing short of incredible . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And the fact almost all of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s GDP expansion depends on such population growth &amp;ldquo ; should be of particular interest to Canadian home builders who are aggressively building homes to try and keep pace with the record number of people moving here . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s of specific concern , Saretsky says in his December report , when many new arrivals are non-permanent residents . &amp;ldquo ; We have become increasingly dependent on the growth of a cohort of people who could quickly vanish in the event of a weakened economy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although the details of the royal couple&amp;rsquo ; s future in Canada are understandably murky &amp;mdash ; including how they might engage in work or what they will do for housing &amp;mdash ; there is no doubt their expressed interest in setting up a part-time home in this country , particularly in B.C. , fits into several new and widespread migration narratives. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MORE RELATED : &lt;/strong&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s what it&amp;rsquo ; s all about . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : 3069 sounds like a lot of classic northern European concept albums from the ' 80s . Was it hard to complete ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;51697;TOOLONG : I like that you used the word concept because it&amp;rsquo ; s like a story , right . The listener who really digs music and focuses on the lyrics will understand how it starts with a guy who experiences a cataclysmic event in his world who is all alone but then ends up having to fight the Dragon Slayer , but he needs help and his buddies come along ( which is the band ) and then meets this woman who is the Queen of the Universe and everything works out in the end . All the songs tell a chapter of that story if you listen to them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;51729;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;51765;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What motivates your writing process , penning a catchy track like With Love on the Line ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;51801;TOOLONG : Is it a tune I want to crank on a Saturday night before I head out with my buddies to give&amp;rsquo ; r ? If the song meets that test , then we keep it . If it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t , me and the band get back to work and keep at it until we know that the song really can give&amp;rsquo ; r and just give it all we got . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Nightseeker is a long way from the garage duo you formed in FUBAR . What was your musical development ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;51833;TOOLONG : I began in a band called Quicksand , but that name was taken so we became Quakesand , and things were going pretty good . But the other three guys were all bricklayers , and one day on the job the scaffolding collapsed when she was a real windy day and they all had various band injuries and I couldn&amp;rsquo ; t wait a year or more for them all to get better . They did , which is good . Then my band in Calgary got hit by lightning and I lost the whole band that night , too , but I was OK . Something about having no testicles seems to make you immune from all those damages , I guess . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What can fans hope to hear in the set ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;51865;TOOLONG : Some old classics , for sure . Out of respect , we don&amp;rsquo ; t play Whale Hunter anymore , after Terry got married to &amp;mdash ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t want to say it &amp;mdash ; the person he married . Mostly , it&amp;rsquo ; s the new album because that&amp;rsquo ; s what everyone wants to hear us play . Most of the crowd is always super wasted , so we usually play about an hour to an hour and a half and then keep give&amp;rsquo ; n it with the fans . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;51897;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;51928;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;51966;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;51995;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;52026;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **31;52068;TOOLONG " &g; ... Stewart talks plant-based eating : It 's not a ' trend'http : **33;52402;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;52437;TOOLONG ... title= " US executive Martha Stewart arrives on the red carpet for the Time 100 Gala at the Lincoln Center in New York on April 23 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " ANGELA WEISS " **28;52473;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1184104 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Guests at &lt;a href= " https : **33;52503;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Martha Stewart&lt;/a&gt; &amp;lsquo ; s holiday party last December were unwittingly served a platter of meatballs . Plant-based meatballs , that is. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I served these meatballs at my Christmas party , and people loved them , &amp;rdquo ; she says of the Beyond Meat Meatballs , which she promotes in a new commercial for the fast-food sandwich chain Subway Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The meat-free meatballs , which are made primarily of pea protein isolate , brown rice and mung bean to form a complete protein , as well as ingredients such as coconut and sunflower oils to keep things together and influence the way the product cooks , were a hit , she says . And nobody noticed that they weren&amp;rsquo ; t actually made of meat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t say anything . I just did it &amp;mdash ; little meatball tidbits &amp;mdash ; and I didn&amp;rsquo ; t say a thing , &amp;rdquo ; Stewart says . &amp;ldquo ; And not one person knew anything , other than they were eating a tasty meatball. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So , was this a bit of holiday season plant-based subterfuge on the part of Stewart ? Not quite , she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was just an experiment on my part to see . Not one person said , &amp;lsquo ; Ew , what is this ? &amp;rsquo ; and put it down , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; And I had **27;52538;TOOLONG of other delicious foods . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52567;TOOLONG ... title= " Martha Stewart stars in a new campaign for Subway Canada highlighting the introduction of Beyond Meat meatballs to its menu . " data-portal-copyright= " Handout " **28;52603;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1184115 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Innovative plant-based products that mimic meaty meals in flavour , appearance and texture like Beyond Meat and Impossible patties are changing the perception of and interest in plant-based foods in North America , the longtime food and lifestyle expert says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; People are very interested , of course they are . Can technology marry with farming and create things that taste the way we are so used to eating and not be meat ? &amp;rdquo ; Stewart says . &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re showing us that , with careful use of ingredients , you can make food extremely tasty and very good for you at the same time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And the momentum of change created by plant-based products and the conversation surrounding them &amp;mdash ; after all , seemingly everyone knows someone who has &amp;lsquo ; gone vegan&amp;rsquo ; within the last year or so &amp;mdash ; is here to stay . The plant-based conversation , Stewart assures skeptics , isn&amp;rsquo ; t merely a &amp;lsquo ; trend&amp;rsquo ; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t think it&amp;rsquo ; s a curiosity . I think it is a definite move toward the future , &amp;rdquo ; Stewart says . &amp;ldquo ; I think more and more of us are understanding about pollution . I think more and more of us are understanding that , if we became a less meat-consuming people , we would be better off . And many studies are pointing out the fact that this is a more healthy way to live . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s a shift in modern diets that Stewart , who released a cookbook of meatless recipes in 2013 , admits she has been watching for years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m very , very curious about the development of the meatless diet . I have been a fan and a friend of ( American author and journalist ) Michael Pollan , I have been a vegetable person my whole life . I am not a vegetarian &amp;mdash ; but I am certainly interested in plant-based foods , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;And I am very interested in improving the pollution levels of the world , and getting rid of big , factory farming would certainly be a step in the right direction . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; " So , if we can cut down on the consumption of too much meat , we would help the planet tremendously . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Stewart admittedly still enjoys a " piece of chicken " or a " pork chop " every now and then -- albeit " rarely . " But she also has vegetarian-minded eaters in her family , a fact she says has influenced hero own outlook on food in recent years . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I have a vegetarian daughter and two pescatarian grandchildren who are very adamant about this , too . &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;rdquo ; And who are very happy about the development of plant-based meat . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;52633;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;52669;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Safe to say that her family was pleased with her latest commercial campaign for Subway Canada , which sees here &amp;ldquo ; grow&amp;rdquo ; plant-based meatballs in a pot of marinara and later &amp;ldquo ; trim&amp;rdquo ; them from the bountifully meatball-covered plant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Oh , they were so thrilled with this commercial . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;55969;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171260 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; BINNERS&amp;rsquo ; DINNER : In fact , a fourth annual lunch , not dinner , was served recently at Regional Recycling Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Evans Street facility . Binners , who constitute 40 per cent of the operation&amp;rsquo ; s 200-to-300 daily clients , also chose from racks of donated warm garments . General manager Jason Smith said that non-binner depositors had waived $2,700 worth of refunds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55999;TOOLONG ... title= " Regional Recycling Vancouver staffer Hen Lai and sister Kim managed the hotdog barbecue at the Evans Street operation 's annual luncheon for binners. The recent doubling of some recycling fees has benefited binners , said Smith , who hopes to see deposits charged on dairy containers . Regional Recycling opens at 7:30 for binners , always provides hot coffee , and conducts a $100 cash draw every two weeks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56065;TOOLONG ... title= " Jane Durante will design a Japanese garden for architect Michael Green 's Salt Spring Island home , art studio and woodworking-shop complex . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;56101;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171259 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; STILL BUILDING : Architect Michael Green threw another seasonal party recently . As usual , its invitation uniquely read : &amp;ldquo ; No RSVP . Guests Welcome . &amp;rdquo ; Wall-to-wall celebrants included landscape architect Janet Durante , who has a home adjacent to Green&amp;rsquo ; s two-hectare Salt Spring Island beachfront property . He has demolished a 1910 house there , retained a 1930s one , and is building a house , art studio and woodworking shop . Durante will add a Japanese-style garden . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s like the shoemaker&amp;rsquo ; s ( unshod ) children , &amp;rdquo ; wood-high-rise specialist Green said of his low-rise complex&amp;rsquo ; s leisurely development . Real-life youngsters , grads and even architectural retirees will benefit from its completion , though . Up to 25 students in Green&amp;rsquo ; s seven-year-old DBR ( Design Build Research ) non-profit program will spend two weeks there . They&amp;rsquo ; ll design and then build structures that , like their Lower Mainland predecessors , will benefit the local community . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56131;TOOLONG ... title= " The UBCP/ACTRA showbiz union named Camille Sullivan and Ben Cotton best actor and actress for roles in films Kingsway and Crown and Anchor . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;56167;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171256 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; AND THE WINNERS ARE : Being honoured by your professional peers is always rewarding . Cotton was cited for his role in the 2018 film Crown and Anchor , and Sullivan for hers in Bruce Sweeney&amp;rsquo ; s city-shot Kingsway. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56197;TOOLONG ... title= " Canadian Club of Vancouver president Raymond Greenwood wore a vintage Vancouver Canucks jersey to welcome former goaltender Kirk McLean . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;56233;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171257 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; TWINE MINDED : Few Vancouver Canucks jerseys are seen at Canadian Club of Vancouver luncheons . But some were worn when club president Raymond Greenwood &amp;mdash ; a.k.a . Mr. Fireworks &amp;mdash ; had &amp;shy ; Kirk McLean reminisce on his 1987&amp;mdash ; 1998 spell as Canucks&amp;rsquo ; goaltender . There were fireworks aplenty when a riot followed McLean backstopping the 1994 Stanley Cup&amp;rsquo ; s seventh-game loss to his future team , the New York Rangers . Of the Canucks today , McLean said : &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re drafting and we&amp;rsquo ; re developing within . &amp;rdquo ; Meanwhile , the team-owning Aquilinis &amp;ldquo ; do everything they can to make players more comfortable . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56263;TOOLONG ... title= " Daniel and Henrik Sedin 's Vancouver Canucks numbers will join Pavel Bure , Trevor Linden , Markus Naslund and Stan Smyl 's when retired in February . " alt= " Laura Byspalko heads a campaign for programming , funding artists and other needs of the Indian Summer Festival that she and Sirish Rao founded in 2011. " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;56365;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171255 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; FESTIVAL SUPPORT : The Indian Summer Festival has gained much status since Laura Byspalko and Sirish Rao founded it in 2011 . According to Rao , the diversified 14-day festival serves &amp;ldquo ; the curious mind . The more it is fed , the more curious it gets . &amp;rdquo ; Byspalko recently launched a campaign for feeding the festival itself with $25&amp;ndash ; $600 donations that would help expand programming , pay artists , keep ticket prices low and provide free access to the needy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; GIVE US AN E : Pacific Central station&amp;rsquo ; s trains and buses must be speedier than repairs to its red-neon sign&amp;rsquo ; s darkened E. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; FISH FINGER-POPPERS : A Fraser River rockfall is preventing many salmon from proceeding upstream . In the Oct. 22 , 1927 , Vancouver Sun , riverside observers blamed a similar dearth on salmon disliking jazz , possibly including Bessie Smith&amp;rsquo ; s then-current Back Water Blues . Conversely , Australia&amp;rsquo ; s Macquarie University Fish Lab recently reported that feeding sharks prefer jazz to classical airs such as Debussy&amp;rsquo ; s La Mer. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56395;TOOLONG ... title= " Lola Lush vamped with city painter Jim Cummins , aka I. Braineater , whose name and art echo currently exhibiting Thierry Guetta , aka Mr. Brainwash. " alt= " Lola Lush vamped with city painter Jim Cummins , aka I. Braineater , whose name and art echo currently exhibiting Thierry Guetta , aka Mr. Brainwash. " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;56431;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171258 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; BRAIN POWER : Chali-Rosso gallerist Susanna Strem is exhibiting works by Mr. Brainwash &amp;mdash ; real name Thierry Guetta &amp;mdash ; at a McArthurGlen Designer Outlet pop-up locale . Some say the Franco-Californian artist is a creation of Britain&amp;rsquo ; s similar Banksy , whose real name may be Robin Gunningham . Vancouver has a brain-nicknamed painter in their genre , too . He&amp;rsquo ; s Jim Cummins , a.k.a . I. Braineater , who covers more canvas , wood , metal and even human skin than many house painters do walls . Perhaps Strem will show him one day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; MISSED CHANCE : One year ago , an all-Canadian Christmas present to Juan Guaid&amp;oacute ; might have changed his lot . Remember him ? Venezuela&amp;rsquo ; s presidential aspirant who , despite many nations&amp;rsquo ; encouragement , couldn&amp;rsquo ; t topple Nicol&amp;aacute ; s Maduro ? If only we&amp;rsquo ; d given him Lt.-Col D.J . Goodspeed&amp;rsquo ; s astounding book , The Conspirators . It analyzes four coups d&amp;rsquo ; etat that succeeded and four that failed , from Belgrade in 1903 to Rastenburg in 1944 . While noting that &amp;ldquo ; no ( coup ) technique yet known could succeed in a true democracy , &amp;rdquo ; The Conspirators&amp;rsquo ; most vital conclusion is : &amp;ldquo ; The nation&amp;rsquo ; s armed forces should support the rebels or at the least be neutral . &amp;rdquo ; Venezuelan troops did neither , whereupon Guaido lost his bid but , remarkably , not his life . Vancouver Public Library has one copy of Goodspeed&amp;rsquo ; s appraisal of a subject necessarily never taught in nations&amp;rsquo ; military staff colleges , so you may need a coup de chance to get it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; DOWN PARRYSCOPE : Merry Christmas to everyone , including post-NAFTA negotiators who gave us the unpronounceable USMCA instead of CAMUS OR USCAM. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;56461;TOOLONG " **34;56490;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 604-929-8456 &lt;/p&gt; Malcolm ParrySat , 21 Dec 2019 04:18:27 **45;56526;TOOLONG ... Perhaps the original Thurlow-off-Robson joint&amp;rsquo ; s 1948 Chrysler taxi will trundle up the Sea to Sky Highway to park outside the new one . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56770;TOOLONG ... title= " Omnifilm Entertainment partner-executive producer Gabriela Schonbach feted daughter Amanda Giannakos on founding separate-but-linked NM Media Co. In the Yaletown&amp;rsquo ; s Earls loft recently , Fresh Roots ( freshroots.ca ) executive director Marc Schutzbank , 32 , received a $13,000 pot swelled by attendees and McNish&amp;rsquo ; s friend Martin Jones , the San Jose Sharks goaltender . Schutzbank , whose wife Ilana Labow co-founded Fresh Roots in 2009 , said elementary and secondary students grow food at educational farms on several schools&amp;rsquo ; grounds , learn to cook it and see it go to cafeterias , food-access programs and suchlike . &amp;ldquo ; When kids are outside and growing something , they also find success in the classroom , and their confidence increases , &amp;rdquo ; said U.S.-native Schutzbank who came to UBC in 2010 on a Fulbright scholarship . &amp;ldquo ; If they grow it , they will eat it , &amp;rdquo ; he said of Fresh Roots&amp;rsquo ; students . Canada , he added , &amp;ldquo ; is the only G7 nation without a federal school-meals program . &amp;rdquo ; He and Labow have a first and presumably healthy-eating child due Jan. 11. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56902;TOOLONG ... title= " After running her artisan-designer Address show here since 2015 , founder Kate Duncan will present 12 designers at Toronto 's DesignTO festival . Having gained a solid reputation , she&amp;rsquo ; ll present 12 of them during Toronto&amp;rsquo ; s DesignTO festival Jan. 14-19. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56968;TOOLONG ... title= " Greeting Matteo Escoto , nine , at an Omega reception , George Frankel said that his family-owned Bridges restaurant will undergo a $15-million renovation.
