" data-portal-copyright= " NETFLIX SCREENGRAB " **28;70395;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1128071 " /&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Vancouver Harbour&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Following their doughnut stop , Chang and Rogen hop a fishing charter with Captain Jason Assonitis with &lt;a href= " https : //www.bonchovy.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Bon Chovy Fishing Charters&lt;/a&gt; to fish for Chinook and trap Dungeness crabs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The pair wax poetic about city&amp;rsquo ; s Seawall , whether the Vancouver Canucks will ever win a Stanley Cup or if the city&amp;rsquo ; s just really good at rioting , and Rogen&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " https : **25;70425;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;non-profit group Hilarity for Charity&lt;/a&gt; with wife Lauren , which raises money for Alzheimer&amp;rsquo ; s research and care . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;70452;TOOLONG ... title= " Chang holds up a Dungeness crab . " alt= " Chang holds up a Dungeness crab . " data-portal-copyright= " NETFLIX SCREENGRAB " **28;70488;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1128160 " /&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Sun Sui Wah Seafood ( 3888 Main St. ) &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lunch takes the pair to Mount Pleasant where they stop in the popular Sun Sui Wah Seafood Restaurant for dim sum , including chicken feet . The dish is often a polarizing one , even for those who grew up around dim sum , let alone anyone who isn&amp;rsquo ; t familiar with Chinese cuisine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Would I have ticked it off ? Not necessarily , &amp;rdquo ; says Rogen with a hearty laugh , but admitting that he enjoys it now and then . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The actor also shares that it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t until he saw The Breakfast Club that he realized ethnic food isn&amp;rsquo ; t commonplace for everyone the way it is in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Molly Ringwald&amp;rsquo ; s character is eating sushi and everyone&amp;rsquo ; s looking at her like she&amp;rsquo ; s a f-cking alien and I remember me and my sister were like , &amp;lsquo ; What&amp;rsquo ; s weird about that ? Like , everyone eats sushi , ' &amp;rdquo ; says Rogen. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;70518;TOOLONG ... title= " Chang ( left ) and Rogen visit Sun Sui Wah in Vancouver , B.C. " alt= " Chang ( left ) and Rogen visit Sun Sui Wah in Vancouver , B.C. " data-portal-copyright= " NETFLIX SCREENGRAB " **28;70554;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1128107 " /&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Van Dusen Gardens ( 5251 Oak St. ) &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Following a post-lunch toke , Rogen takes Chang to Van Dusen Gardens where the pair attempt to complete a hedge maze . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We would do mushrooms and go to this hedge maze , &amp;rdquo ; Rogen confesses of his younger years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The hedge maze is one of only six in North American and consists of some 3,000 pyramidal cedars planted in 1981 , according to &lt;a href= " https : **33;70584;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Atlas Obscura&lt;/a&gt; . The pair manage to make it through the maze but not without a few wrong turns and copious amounts of laughter . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The garden is &lt;a href= " http : **34;70619;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;open year-round and admission&lt;/a&gt; starts at $8 for adults . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;70655;TOOLONG ... title= " The pair are turned around in the Van Dusen Gardens hedge maze in an episode of Chang 's new Netflix series , Breakfast , Lunch &amp; Dinner . " alt= " The pair are turned around in the Van Dusen Gardens hedge maze in an episode of Chang 's new Netflix series , Breakfast , Lunch &amp; Dinner . " data-portal-copyright= " NETFLIX SCREENGRAB " **28;70691;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1128117 " /&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Apna Bhaia ( 6616 Fraser St. ) &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; On their way to their next stop , Rogen swings by his childhood neighbourhood and reveals that his mom Sandy &amp;ndash ; who is &lt;a href= " https : //twitter.com/RogenSandy " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;a hilarious Twitter presence&lt;/a&gt; in her own right &amp;ndash ; was &amp;ldquo ; the worst f-cking cook . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We would go out to eat all the time because we didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to eat my mom&amp;rsquo ; s food , &amp;rdquo ; Rogen admits , before the pair devour samosas outside &lt;a href= " https : //apnabhaia.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Apna Bhaia&lt;/a&gt; and discuss cooking without rules . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;70721;TOOLONG ... title= " Rogen ( left ) and Chang devour samosas on Fraser Street in Vancouver , B.C. " alt= " Rogen ( left ) and Chang devour samosas on Fraser Street in Vancouver , B.C. " data-portal-copyright= " NETFLIX SCREENGRAB " **28;70757;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1128134 " /&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Hong Kong BBQ Master ( 4651 No. 3 Road , Richmond ) &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The episode&amp;rsquo ; s final stop is dinner in Richmond , where some of the region&amp;rsquo ; s best Asian cuisine is found . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The pair head to the underground parkade for Superstore where Hong Kong BBQ Master is tucked away . The eat-in and carry-out spot often boasts line-ups of people hoping to pick up whatever they can from the day&amp;rsquo ; s menu before dishes are sold out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The pair chat with owner Eric Leung and son Anson , who now manages the diner , about the boom of Chinese immigration following the 1997 British handover of Hong Kong to China and why Chinese cooking done well is rare . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chang and Rogen dine on Chinese melon soup , Hong Kong-style honey BBQ pork , crispy roasted pork and soy chicken . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;70787;TOOLONG ... title= " Rogen ( left ) and Chang enter Hong Kong BBQ Master in Richmond , B.C. to cap off their day . " alt= " Rogen ( left ) and Chang enter Hong Kong BBQ Master in Richmond , B.C. to cap off their day . " data-portal-copyright= " NETFLIX SCREENGRAB " **28;70823;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1128152 " /&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Vancouver Aquarium ( 845 Avison Way ) &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; To wrap up their day of toking and snacking through Vancouver , Rogen takes Chang to the Vancouver Aquarium to meet a certain sea creature . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Late last year , the Vancouver Aquarium sought suggestions on what to name its newest Giant Pacific octopus . To no one&amp;rsquo ; s surprise , Rogen &amp;ndash ; who at the time was riding a wave of popularity having spent the summer as the voice of TransLink &amp;ndash ; managed to rally fans to vote for , yes , &amp;ldquo ; Ceph Rogen&amp;rdquo ; to be the cephalopod&amp;rsquo ; s new name . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The episode ends as Rogen and Chang try to feed Ceph Rogen , marvel at jellyfish and what owning a jellyfish tank says about one&amp;rsquo ; s ego , and how Rogen&amp;rsquo ; s love for Vancouver is certainly no act . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You genuinely , no bullsh-t , love this town , &amp;rdquo ; concludes Chang. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I really do . As you see , there&amp;rsquo ; s a lot to love about it . It&amp;rsquo ; s not a hard place to love , &amp;rdquo ; says Rogen. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;70853;TOOLONG ... title= " Rogen points out an anaconda in a display case at the Vancouver Aquarium . " alt= " Rogen points out an anaconda in a display case at the Vancouver Aquarium . " data-portal-copyright= " NETFLIX SCREENGRAB " **28;70889;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1128174 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " mailto:sip@postmedia.com " **31;70919;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **25;70952;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;70979;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; Stephanie IpThu , 24 Oct 2019 00:48:01 **45;71012;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsLocal NewsSPARK Animation conference draws big name players to Hollywood Northhttp : **35;71059;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;71096;TOOLONG ... title= " Chris Buck directed the Walt Disney animated movie Tarzan , which won an Oscar in 1999. " alt= " Chris Buck directed the Walt Disney animated movie Tarzan , which won an Oscar in 1999. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;71132;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1121605 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;SPARK Animation&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;71162;TOOLONG : Oct. 24- 27 , various times &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;71197;TOOLONG : Emily Carr University , 520 E. 1st Ave . and other venues &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : &lt;a href= " http : **32;71233;TOOLONG ? f ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;71267;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 1934 , Walt Disney decided that the studio bearing his name would move into making feature-length animated films and work started on Snow White and the Seven Dwarfs . The first full-length animated movie took years to make and industry insiders were so convinced it would bankrupt the studio , they dubbed the project &amp;ldquo ; Disney&amp;rsquo ; s Folly . &amp;rdquo ; How wrong they were . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For a brief period , the 1937 film was the highest-grossing sound film of all time . It remains in the top-earning animated films to this day , and it&amp;rsquo ; s impossible to separate the Disney brand from the animated feature genre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In subsequent decades , full-length animated movies saw their popularity decline . Then the Disney Renaissance ( 1989 to 1999 ) restored Walt Disney Animation Studios into a global animation powerhouse . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chris Buck was around for that rise , co-directing 1999s Academy Award-winning Tarzan . The Oscar-winning director of 2013s Frozen and the coming sequel Frozen 2 will appear at the 2019 SPARK Animation conference for a 20th anniversary screening of Tarzan on Saturday , preceded by a question and answer with Buck and co-director Kevin Lima . Buck is also a keynote speaker at the conference . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the things the session is certain to touch upon is the way in which the film managed to skirt both young and old audiences alike . This was the source material from author Edgar Rice Burroughs&amp;rsquo ; 1912 novel Tarzan of the Apes writ large in eye-catching animation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; While we always had the kids in mind , we never talked down to them or deliberately did anything that would mess with the well-known original source material , &amp;rdquo ; said Buck . &amp;ldquo ; We had these incredible action sequences that we didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to dilute or lessen the intensity of and that kept it enjoyable for all-ages and really got audiences thrilled with the viewing experience . Of course , we also had the incredible music from Phil Collins . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;71300;TOOLONG ... title= " Oscar-winning Director Chris Buck attends the Frozen Fan Fest Product Showcase at Casita Hollywood this October 02 , 2019 in Los Angeles , California . " alt= " Oscar-winning Director Chris Buck attends the Frozen Fan Fest Product Showcase at Casita Hollywood this October 02 , 2019 in Los Angeles , California . " data-portal-copyright= " Andrew Toth " **28;71336;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1121543 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In fact , it was Collins&amp;rsquo ; soundtrack and song , You&amp;rsquo ; ll Be In My Heart , which won the movie an Oscar , a Golden Globe and a Grammy . The winning Disney formula dating back to classics like Dumbo and the Jungle Book , where a fast-paced classic storyline is paired with show-stopping musical numbers had been upgraded to the modern pop era . Subsequent hits such as Frozen and the Lion King remake have ridden this to massive appeal . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There were so many elements of Tarzan that made it so special , especially to have it be my first feature , &amp;rdquo ; said Buck . &amp;ldquo ; It started over in the TV studio under Kevin Lima&amp;rsquo ; s direction , as he had done a Goofy movie for them . Features got hold of it and took it over , but owing to the size and scope of the project , they wanted Kevin to bring in a second . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Buck was reticent to become involved with the movie , viewing animation as a particularly time-consuming arena of the film biz . He had seen colleagues get bogged down in full-length cartoons and wasn&amp;rsquo ; t sure that the work was for him . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then I was on weekend vacation at a ski hill in a rented condo and they had an old copy of Reader&amp;rsquo ; s Digest in the room that I picked up to thumb through before we hit the slopes , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; And in the margins , someone had written &amp;lsquo ; people regret more of what they haven&amp;rsquo ; t done than what they have done . &amp;rsquo ; There was my answer to my struggle over the decision whether to do Tarzan or not . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;71366;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;71402;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Made for a budget of US$130-plus million , Tarzan was one of the most expensive animated features of the time . To date , worldwide box office proceeds are $450-plus million-and-counting with the 20th anniversary possibly driving sales up again . The Buck-directed Frozen was moderately more expensive ( $150 million ) and took in a staggering $1.2 billion-plus . Frozen held the top-grossing animated box office record until August 2019 when the reboot of the Lion King passed $1.3 billion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Eighty two years on , &amp;ldquo ; Disney&amp;rsquo ; s Folly , &amp;rdquo ; appears to paid off plenty . Frozen 2 lands on Nov. 22. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Like Tarzan being somewhat more mature , in the world of Frozen 2 , we are letting our characters grow up some , &amp;rdquo ; said Buck . &amp;ldquo ; We found the emotion in the adoption story , and being able to give life , emotion and voice to the animals that Tarzan is raised by and lives and communicates with was a beautiful way to go . Animation , particularly Disney , does that type of tale beautifully and , unlike any previous Tarzan film , we could make him move like Burroughs had written it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Naturally , working with source material as familiar as Tarzan enables the creative team a great deal more depth of background to work on the film&amp;rsquo ; s narrative line . There are a total of 24 Tarzan books , the majority of them were written before Burroughs died in 1950 , with subsequent novels being co-written from the author&amp;rsquo ; s ideas or other writers officially authorized by the estate to keep the story going . Buck and the team knew that to keep their cinematic story going , they needed to embrace the latest technology for maximum impact . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;71438;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;71474;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We were able to use this new technique called Deep Canvas , which enabled us to move through the jungle in a way that wasn&amp;rsquo ; t just flat , but instead enabled you to be immersed in the environments you were moving through , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; It was created by some of our incredible artists and technicians and that&amp;rsquo ; s how you get those magic scenes of Tarzan practically surfing through the trees and the animals moving through so fluidly . I still think it looks amazing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Besides the panel discussion to follow the screening of Tarzan , Buck will also be presenting a lifetime achievement award on opening night ( Oct. 24 ) to Hollywood animation production legend Bonnie Arnold . The producer of the How to Train Your Dragon franchise , as well as Toy Story and Tarzan , she has worked with Disney , Pixar and DreamWorks Animation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;71510;TOOLONG " **36;71541;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;71579;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;71608;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynTue , 22 Oct 2019 17:39:06 **45;71639;TOOLONG ... Gladwell 's latest book looks at how we talk to strangershttp : **31;71686;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;71719;TOOLONG ... title= " Author Malcolm Gladwell looks at how we talk and deal with people we do n't know in his new book Talking to Strangers Photo credit : Celeste Sloman " alt= " Author Malcolm Gladwell looks at how we talk and deal with people we do n't know in his new book Talking to Strangers Photo credit : Celeste Sloman " data-portal-copyright= " Celeste Sloman " **28;71755;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1112455 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thanks to the circus-like atmosphere of American politics , best-selling author &lt;a href= " https : //www.gladwellbooks.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Malcolm Gladwell&lt;/a&gt; admits he pines a little for his old job as a Washington Post reporter . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gladwell was at the newspaper from 1987 to 1996 , first as a business and science writer , and then the Post&amp;rsquo ; s New York bureau chief . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I miss it . To be at the Washington Post right now is the best moment to be a journalist in 50 years . It&amp;rsquo ; s fantastic , &amp;rdquo ; said Gladwell , reflecting on the constant mayhem of Donald Trump&amp;rsquo ; s administration . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It must be so thrilling . They must be working 60 hours a week &amp;mdash ; if not more . I am sure they are killing themselves , but this is what journalists live for , this kind of stuff . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You see the value . This is a really , really powerful reminder of how valuable a free press is . Democracy can not function without that , &amp;rdquo ; he added . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The topic of American politics came up ( as it often does these days ) in advance of the five-time best-selling author&amp;rsquo ; s event at the &lt;a href= " https : //writersfest.bc.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancouver Writers Festival&lt;/a&gt; &amp;nbsp ; to discuss his new book , &lt;a href= " https : **33;71785;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Talking to Strangers : What We Should Know about the People We Do n't Know&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;71820;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;71856;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the book , Gladwell reminds us of the polarized world we live in , how we have stopped listening to each other , and how we often make the wrong assumptions about others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the more impactful points in the new book is the idea that we don&amp;rsquo ; t have to meet a person to understand them . In fact , it might be better , at first , if we went on hard facts over handshakes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A classic example of that theory , Gladwell says , is former British Prime Minister Neville Chamberlain&amp;rsquo ; s huge hoodwinking at the hands of Adolf Hitler before Second World War . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The people who were wrong about Hitler were the ones who had talked with him for hours , &amp;rdquo ; said Gladwell , who outlines Chamberlain&amp;rsquo ; s trips to meet with Hitler in the book . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those who were right about him never met him , he says , but they read the writing on the wall and the writing in Mein Kampf ( the 1925 manifesto where Hitler laid out his anti-Semitic political ideology and future plans ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gladwell&amp;rsquo ; s course toward his latest book began with an interest in spies , and how many of them were inept bumblers who somehow went go undetected in the high-stakes world of international espionage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t understand why it was so hard to catch spies . So I did all that spy stuff that&amp;rsquo ; s in the book first , but I didn&amp;rsquo ; t think it was a book . I just thought it was interesting . I did all those interviews and I went down and met ( psychologist ) Tim Levine , &amp;rdquo ; said Gladwell. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; ( Levine ) has thought as much about the problem of why we are deceived by strangers as anyone in social science , &amp;rdquo ; added Gladwell about the professor and chair of Communication Studies at the University of Alabama Birmingham . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of Levine&amp;rsquo ; s ideas is that we &amp;ldquo ; default to truth&amp;rdquo ; as basically a way of just getting on with things . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Levine&amp;rsquo ; s big argument is trusting strangers makes for a very efficient communication and social transaction &amp;mdash ; default to truth . The reason why we have evolved to default to truth is that it just works better . It allows us to do all kinds of things , &amp;rdquo ; said Gladwell , who was raised in rural Ontario but has called New York City home for years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With his spy research completed , Gladwell decided to move forward on a book after the story of Sandra Bland broke . Bland was in Texas when she was pulled over for failing to signal a lane change . The cop demanded she get out of the car . She questioned why , and they argued . He eventually said : &amp;ldquo ; I will light you up ! Get out ! Now ! &amp;rdquo ; He drew his gun . She got out and went to jail where she took her own life three days later . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know where I was going with it and then Sandra Bland happened and I realized that was a kind of really , really useful way of coming to grips with this problem of these sort of tragic encounters between law enforcement and African-Americans . And so Sandra Bland was the catalyst for the book , but I had been kind of thinking about this problem of strangers before that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thanks to the internet , we interact with strangers all the time . Modernity has moved us outside of what Gladwell calls our &amp;ldquo ; kinship circle , &amp;rdquo ; and forced us to confront people who are different . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If you think about things like Airbnb and Uber , they&amp;rsquo ; re two exercises in institutionalized stranger transactions , where you&amp;rsquo ; re taking an extraordinary leap of faith entrusting your home and your transportation to someone you have never met . Someone you basically know nothing about and who supposedly was kind of vetted by some third party &amp;mdash ; but who even knows if that is the case ? &amp;rdquo ; said Gladwell. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We weren&amp;rsquo ; t doing that in the 1960s , were we ? We weren&amp;rsquo ; t even doing that in 1985 . So , there is something weird about that going on right now . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Doing research for the book and talking to strangers Gladwell says reminded him to take a beat before forming a definitive opinion about another person . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It has powerfully reinforced something that I might have had already &amp;mdash ; a kind of caution about&amp;nbsp ; drawing conclusions about people I don&amp;rsquo ; t know well . It is something I have always done , but it is something I feel more strongly about , &amp;rdquo ; said Gladwell. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I have lost all my sense of certainty about my ability to make sense of someone . Now I am just trying to reserve judgment as much as possible . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That outlook comes in handy when you are out on the road promoting a book , meeting new people every day and having your work critiqued and criticized by those who aren&amp;rsquo ; t buying what you&amp;rsquo ; re selling . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I have done it before , I know what to expect . It is always exhausting but it is exhilarating , as well , &amp;rdquo ; said Gladwell about the launch of a new book . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; With book one , I was , predictably , much more sensitive to criticism than I am now . Now , not really . I&amp;rsquo ; ve been doing this for 20 years , and I have built an audience . I have painstakingly cultivated a group of people who like what I do and want to participate in my ( work ) &amp;mdash ; whether it is my podcast or my books , my articles or whatever . Those are the people I am writing books for . So that is more than enough for me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t need 100 per cent approval . I don&amp;rsquo ; t think anybody gets a 100 per cent approval , do they ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With the book already launched , Gladwell is looking forward to focusing on the fourth season of his podcast &lt;a href= " http : **25;71886;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Revisionist History&lt;/a&gt; . While he enjoys the format of podcasts , he admits it makes for a lot more creative anxiety than writing a book . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I need 10 ideas , not one , for the podcast . It&amp;rsquo ; s a huge difference . It is actually a little more hair-raising than writing a book , &amp;rdquo ; said Gladwell. &amp;ldquo ; I am having a good time , but I am losing sleep . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " dgee@postmedia.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;71913;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;71947;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;71978;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;72020;TOOLONG ... more about books and **28;72056;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;72086;TOOLONG ... the latest in local , national and international books **25;72122;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;72149;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? The fraudster tells his victims he needs the cash to cover things like airfare and passport fees . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A romance scam , as defined by Crime Stoppers , is a fraud that occurs when an individual demonstrates false romantic intentions toward a victim in order to gain their trust and affection for the purpose of obtaining their money or access to their bank accounts or credit cards . NewsTown Talk : A Night To Dream gala benefits expanding Ronald McDonald Househttp : **35;73249;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;73286;TOOLONG ... title= " Seen with **25;73322;TOOLONG Terri-Lynn Williams-Davidson , artist-carver and Order of Canada member Robert Davidson is the subject of director Charles Wilkinson 's feature-length documentary , Haida Modern . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;73376;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1117493 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; SWEET DREAMING : Ronald McDonald House&amp;rsquo ; s recent A Night to Dream gala was a recurring one for Lindsey Turner , who chaired it for the fourth consecutive time . The 17th annual event reportedly grossed $680,000 to help accommodate the 2,000-a-year families who occupy the 73-suite facility for an average 13-day stay . CEO Richard Pass and new board chair Patrick McGuinty may soon announce that up to 52 suites will be added to five-year-old Ronald McDonald House on the B.C. Children&amp;rsquo ; s Hospital campus . Four-bedroom satellites are also expected beside Royal Columbian Hospital and Royal Inland Hospital in Kamloops . They&amp;rsquo ; ll duplicate one at Surrey Memorial Hospital . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;73406;TOOLONG ... title= " Ronald McDonald House CEO Richard Pass and four-time Night of Dreams gala chair Lindsey Turner saw that event reportedly grossed $680,000. " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;73442;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1117490 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; MODEL CITIZEN : Masset-raised artist Robert Davidson is the subject of Charles Wilkinson&amp;rsquo ; s documentary , Haida Modern , that premiered during the recent Vancouver International Film Festival . Called &amp;ldquo ; a prot&amp;eacute ; g&amp;eacute ; and friend&amp;rdquo ; by celebrated late carver Bill Reid , Davidson also perceives the Haida tradition not as inviolable rules but as the basis for evolving , living art . His own wide-ranging artworks include gold coins that the Canadian Mint released to accompany his 1997 elevation to the Order of Canada . $50,000 in ordinary currency came his way in 2010 with the Audain Prize for Lifetime Achievement . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve been thinking about a new car , &amp;rdquo ; the ever-modest Davidson said before cheerfully admitting that he&amp;rsquo ; d forwarded the entire amount to fund post-secondary bursaries for Haida Gwaii students . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;73472;TOOLONG ... title= " Former B.C. Such events have been staged annually since 13 Italian associations founded the Slocan-at-Grandview &amp;ldquo ; Il Centro&amp;rdquo ; on a 3.25-hectare former city dump site . This year , Cuccione inducted former B.C. Lions football team head coach and general manager Wally Buono into the centre&amp;rsquo ; s Hall of Fame . Happily , his old team defeated the Toronto Argonauts 55-8 the following day . Buono likely approved the teamwork when catering director Fabio Rasotto&amp;rsquo ; s kitchen squad served the centre&amp;rsquo ; s fourth full-capacity banquet that week , then repeated it the following night when the Confratellanza Italo-Canadese Society honoured longtime community benefactor John DeLucchi. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;73538;TOOLONG ... title= " Susan Mendelson celebrated her Lazy Gourmet catering firm 's 40th anniversary made possible by her policy of hiring " people better than me. She likely didn&amp;rsquo ; t foresee that when a UBC arts-and-social-work degree scored her a $350-a-month job at the Vancouver East Cultural Centre , now the Cultch . To meet her rent , she made carrot cake , cheesecake and Nanaimo bars for sale during intervals . She and friend Deborah Roitberg then founded Lazy Gourmet , but Mendelson&amp;rsquo ; s brush with dramatics continued . That was when &amp;ldquo ; two beat-up cars jammed in ( a departing customer ) and all these scruffy-looking people were waving guns . &amp;rdquo ; Suspecting that it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t part of an earlier movie shoot , Mendelson asked if she should call the cops . &amp;ldquo ; We &lt;em&gt;are &lt;/em&gt; the cops , &amp;rdquo ; one fracas member replied . Macy , during a weekend stroll through Downtown Vancouver on the weekend . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her only previous acting credit was as an extra in the 2017 Vancouver-filmed feature The Layover , which was directed by her dad . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her mother , Academy Award-nominated actress Felicity Huffman , was sentenced last month to 14 days in prison after pleading guilty to conspiracy related to her payment of $15,000 to have someone secretly correct answers her daughter Sophia provided on the SAT standardized test used for college admissions. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Huffman was one of 51 parents collared in the U.S. federal investigation into a far-reaching criminal conspiracy that helped privileged kids with middling grades gain admission to elite U.S. universities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver businessman &lt;a href= " https : **33;74114;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;David Sidoo&lt;/a&gt; , actress &lt;a href= " https : **33;74149;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Lori Loughlin&lt;/a&gt; and Surrey woman &lt;a href= " https : **33;74184;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Xiaoning Sui&lt;/a&gt; have also been charged . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **33;74219;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Huffman , the first parent to be sentenced in the scandal , will begin her two-week prison term Oct. 25. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;74254;TOOLONG " **34;74283;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **26;74319;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;74347;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;74381;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? NewsAudible.ca releases hip-hop industry insider Sophia Chang 's The Baddest Bitch in the Roomhttp : **36;74890;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;74928;TOOLONG ... title= " Sophia Chang attends Bustle 's 2019 Rule Breakers Festival at the LeFrak Center at Lakeside on Sept. 21 in Brooklyn , N.Y . " alt= " Sophia Chang attends Bustle 's 2019 Rule Breakers Festival at the LeFrak Center at Lakeside on Sept. 21 in Brooklyn , N.Y . " data-portal-copyright= " Bryan Bedder " **28;74964;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1101816 " /&gt; **27;74994;TOOLONG Baddest Bitch in the **30;75023;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Sept. 26 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Online &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; $14.95 a month . First month free at &lt;a href= " https : **33;75055;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;audible.ca&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s hardly news that the music industry is still white-male dominated and minorities , particularly female , are barely represented in its ranks . When she moved from her hometown of Vancouver to New York in 1988 to pursue a career in music , Sophia Chang didn&amp;rsquo ; t give that a single thought . The city was the heartbeat of hip-hop . The 23-year-old Korean-Canadian was enchanted by the genre ever since a classmate at Prince of Wales mini-school spun Grandmaster Flash and the Furious Five&amp;rsquo ; s breakout release , The Message . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chang planned on taking a big bite out of Big Apple rap . She did that and a whole lot more , becoming a major music-biz player privately as well as producing runway shows for Vivienne Tam and the Project Runway All Stars , and writing a screenplay for HBO . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In her audible.ca memoir titled The Baddest Bitch in the Room , Chang tells the tale of how someone who grew up in super-safe , suburban , white Vancouver became an A&amp;amp ; R ( artist and repertoire , talent scout ) for the enormously influential label Jive and moved in the grittier circles of Staten Island backroom recording studios hanging out with Wu-Tang Clan founder RZA and opening up her Manhattan apartment to a who&amp;rsquo ; s who of hip-hop legends to crash in . While she may not be a household name , Chang&amp;rsquo ; s name is familiar to any in the music industry during rap&amp;rsquo ; s golden age , and the journey she details in her audiobook comes across like a script for a biopic that is coming to a streaming service near you . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her memories of life in Manhattan&amp;rsquo ; s Lower East Side in the heyday of hip-hop , new wave , the loft scene and more is loaded with sharp insights into what life was like in the days before gentrification and development turned the Bowery into a boutique . No , it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t always wonderful , but it certainly was real . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know that The Message was what I wanted to hear more than anything else until I heard it . I couldn&amp;rsquo ; t identify that missing thing until I heard it and it was so real , &amp;rdquo ; said Chang. &amp;ldquo ; We were listening to The Jam , The Clash and the Pistols a little bit , with this urgency and **26;75090;TOOLONG . Then there was this hip-hop with all of that and this phenomenal storytelling like nothing I had ever heard before , plus that incredible beat . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Naturally , her guitar-playing boyfriend at the time said it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t real music . This tired-out line is still trotted out by unoriginal musicians everywhere who resent talented turntablists and MCs making music that matters rather than trying to reformat Foghat for commercial rock radio . Tied into this whole attitude , which goes back to such loathsome acts as Disco Demolition Night at Comiskey Park , Chicago , on July 12 , 1979 , are elements of racism , homophobia and class bias . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;75118;TOOLONG ... title= " Sophia Chang attends Bustle 's 2019 Rule Breakers Festival at LeFrak Center in Brooklyn on Sept. 21. " alt= " Sophia Chang attends Bustle 's 2019 Rule Breakers Festival at LeFrak Center in Brooklyn on Sept. 21. " data-portal-copyright= " Bryan Bedder " **28;75154;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1109001 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I worked closely with Nile Rodgers ( Chic ) who was huge in the disco era . And in his phenomenal biography , The Freak , he talks about how the music came right out of the black-and-brown queer scene in the New York underground in the &amp;rsquo ; 70s , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; And a lot of that opposition to disco came from racism and homophobia , and a lot of the resistance to hip-hop , for sure , had to do with race . I absolutely think that if rap had been coming from white men , there wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t have been that pushback. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As she notes in the book , there was a point where that opposition to hip-hop as a musical force became tilting at windmills . Soundscan&amp;rsquo ; s sales chart couldn&amp;rsquo ; t be denied . The genre was exploding on vinyl , on select radio stations , on MTV and in concert . Sales figures for albums like the first NWA or Wu-Tang Clan&amp;rsquo ; s Enter the Wu-Tang ( 36 Chambers ) mirrored those of breakout rock bands . Working with artists finding a way to describe their culture in a powerful voice led Chang back to her own issues of cultural identity and belonging . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My mother and father literally risked their lives to get out of Korea and I was raised with a serious amount of , what I&amp;rsquo ; ll call , their intrepidity , and I knew that I was smart , that my opinion mattered , and I didn&amp;rsquo ; t take it much further than that , &amp;rdquo ; said Chang. &amp;ldquo ; Coming into a genre that was so clearly defined by gender and by race was daunting for me , but hip-hop embraced me so completely . I came to proclaim that , &amp;lsquo ; My name is Sophia Chang and I was raised by Wu-Tang , &amp;rsquo ; because it was so . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She spent decades working with Wu-Tang Clan&amp;rsquo ; s RZA , GZA and ODB and was involved with A Tribe Called Quest , Raphael Saadiq and D&amp;rsquo ; Angelo as well . Through this continuing connection , she had what is described as a &amp;ldquo ; transformational odyssey , &amp;rdquo ; coming to a point where she took what she learned from her dear friends and applied it to her own life . Along the way , she would take up Shaolin martial arts and have children with an actual practising monk , move up the ranks of record-label boardrooms and come to define her role as an Asian woman in the entertainment business . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; One of the best things about this job has been that I&amp;rsquo ; ve been able to not only work with these artists , but also to come to experience their humanity and learned from them , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; For years , people have been saying that I should write a book about my &amp;lsquo ; crazy life , &amp;rsquo ; and I bristled at the idea because it would be me writing about hanging out and working with famous people , and who gives a shit . But when I started working at Universal Music Group in 2014 and was mentoring a bunch of really young women , I realized that telling my story and relating my experiences could be in service of others trying to make it in this business . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chang said working with Paul Simon made her acutely aware of the price people pay for fame . Her late mentor Sonya Chang told her to fiercely protect her anonymity for both longevity and sanity . By bringing her life out of the boardroom and into a book , Chang bares it all , warts and everything , proclaiming , &amp;ldquo ; I am the fearsome empress on the throne , to whom you bend the knee and whose bling you kiss . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Baddest Bitch in the Room is 54 now . She says releasing an Audible Original was an obvious choice in storytelling for someone who has held onto microphones for decades . Chang is currently developing film and TV properties , raising her children and trains kung fu seven days a week . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;75184;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;75220;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;75256;TOOLONG " **36;75287;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;75325;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;75354;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynSun , 29 Sep 2019 18:00:01 **45;75385;TOOLONG ... Wilson-Raybould is on the campaign trail with a new book and no partyhttp : **35;75432;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;75469;TOOLONG ... title= " Independent candidate for Vancouver Granville , Jody Wilson-Raybould holds up a copy of her new book . " alt= " Independent candidate for Vancouver Granville , Jody Wilson-Raybould holds up a copy of her new book . " data-portal-copyright= " NICK PROCAYLO " **28;75505;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1100616 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Let&amp;rsquo ; s just get it out of the way , former justice minister and attorney general &lt;a href= " https : **33;75535;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Jody **25;75570;TOOLONG will be writing a memoir . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But no , she won&amp;rsquo ; t be releasing it a few days before the Oct. 21 federal election . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m not going to throw a bomb unless a bomb is to work really hard and work on behalf of your constituents , then maybe , &amp;rdquo ; said Wilson-Raybould when asked if she would use the &lt;a href= " https : //www.snclavalin.com/en " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; SNC-Lavalin&lt;/a&gt; affair to deliver a blow to her former boss , Justin Trudeau , and the Liberal party . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;75597;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;75633;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While there is no timeline yet for the impending memoir , Wilson-Raybould admits she has had it on her mind for a while now . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I have lots of notebooks and I have been writing about my experiences , &amp;rdquo ; added the former Liberal cabinet member and We Wai Kai Nation member . &amp;ldquo ; From what I can tell , people are interested in it , so I will write a memoir at some point . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Oh yes , people are interested . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the meantime , Wilson-Raybould has released &lt;a href= " https : **33;75663;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;From Where I Stand : Rebuilding Indigenous Nations for a Stronger Canada . &lt;/a&gt; It doesn&amp;rsquo ; t include any deep dives , it&amp;rsquo ; s more a matter of Wilson-Raybould dipping a toe into the SNC-Lavalin affair that saw her resign on principle from cabinet in February . Two months later she , along with MP Jane Philpott , was ejected from the Liberal caucus . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The book instead is made up of an extensive introduction followed by a series of speeches she has given over the years . The opening speech is a keynote to the Western Canada Aboriginal Law Forum in May of 2010 . The book closes with her speaking at a Feminists Deliver event in June of 2019. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Driving those speeches is the theme of reconciliation , including the understanding of how we got to where we are on Indigenous issues as well as ideas on structured , sensitive and , most importantly , viable paths forward to ensure true reconciliation . She says the Liberal government could have done more to advance reconciliation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wilson-Raybould , who was the first Indigenous person to be attorney general , asks the citizenry ( including government , Indigenous leaders and the public ) to help continue to move reconciliation forward . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I envision 10 years from now we will have taken bold leadership and created the mechanisms to transform Indigenous nations or to create the space for Indigenous nations to be self-determining , including self-governing , &amp;rdquo ; said Wilson-Raybould. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wilson-Raybould grew up in Port Hardy and Comox . Her Kwak&amp;rsquo ; wala name is Puglaas , which roughly translates to &amp;ldquo ; woman born to noble people . &amp;rdquo ; Her father is Bill Wilson , a First Nations hereditary chief , politician , and lawyer . Wilson-Raybould is a former crown prosecutor and she was Regional Chief of the Assembly of First Nations for B.C. ( 2009-2015 ) before entering federal politics and becoming a star candidate for the Liberals in 2015. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , that all changed when news broke that the PMO had allegedly attempted to influence Wilson-Raybould in regards to the ongoing prosecution of SNC-Lavalin . After appearing in front of the House Judiciary Committee , Wilson-Raybould became a household name &amp;mdash ; and a major thorn in the side of the prime minister . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;75698;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver Granville independent candidate Jody Wilson-Raybould. " alt= " Vancouver Granville independent candidate Jody Wilson-Raybould. " data-portal-copyright= " Mike Bell " **28;75734;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1101274 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now , here we are month away from a federal election and Wilson-Raybould &amp;mdash ; who was stripped of her Liberal party nomination for the 2019 federal election &amp;mdash ; is promoting a book and running to retain her Vancouver-Granville riding . The Liberals have thrown their weight and money behind candidate Taleeb Noormohamed. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was a difficult period of time for almost a year now . I think though through life , and this reality I have lived through , you learn lessons , &amp;rdquo ; said Wilson-Raybould , who was also briefly the veterans affairs minister . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; People don&amp;rsquo ; t bring up the specific facts of that company in Quebec ( SNC Lavalin ) but they bring it up to me and are having discussions of the very nature of our democracy . It is so surprising , but so welcoming , to have people talk about the importance of the rule of law and why we are so fortunate to live in a country like this because we have the rule of law and we uphold it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think that everything happens for a reason . Now I am running as an independent and I&amp;rsquo ; m in the place that I should be , &amp;rdquo ; added Wilson-Raybould. &amp;ldquo ; The fact that people have learned similar lessons and have that greater awareness of what they want from politics , or that they want to have politicians make decisions on what&amp;rsquo ; s important in terms of the issues versus what&amp;rsquo ; s important in terms of political expediency or power is why I got involved in politics in the first place to actually &amp;mdash ; and it&amp;rsquo ; s an over used phrase &amp;mdash ; do politics differently . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It really has emboldened me. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver Granville is easily going to be one of the most-watched races in B.C. And maybe even the country . Wilson-Raybould says she is feeling good and more importantly feeling the support . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The idea of Wilson-Raybould running as an independent raises some red flags for people , most importantly will her voice be heard over a chorus of party-backed and party-line towing MPs ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m in the right place at this time , &amp;rdquo ; said Wilson-Raybould. &amp;ldquo ; I have always advocated my own views , beliefs and principals . I&amp;rsquo ; m not tied to or told what to say which I think is the extremely positive element of being an independent . Less partisanship is what I hear all the time . People actually just want to talk about issues . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wilson-Raybould wants to , and hopes her constituents and the rest of the country can take a minute and look at her and her campaign as a way forward , a way to begin to loosen up the partisan political party lines that many believe have tied up our democratic system . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What we need politicians in Ottawa , actually anywhere for that matter , to do says Wilson-Raybould is to take a page from the Indigenous people&amp;rsquo ; s playbook. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I found and learned that Ottawa could take some lessons in terms of decision-making from the culture that I come from , &amp;rdquo ; said Wilson-Raybould. &amp;ldquo ; All voices are important . That&amp;rsquo ; s the culture I come from . You have to raise people up to fulfil their roles and if you don&amp;rsquo ; t do that there is something taken away from the strength of the community . People might roll their eyes when I say something like that and it might sound airy-fairy or naive but in my view it&amp;rsquo ; s not . My culture has survived for millennia . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I am not saying parliament is going to change overnight and we&amp;rsquo ; re all going to function by way of consensus , but at least moving in that direction or recognizing that we are never going to resolve issues like climate change or Indigenous reconciliation unless we come together and have bold plans and understand in having bold plans they have to live beyond the life of one government in order to be sustainable in order to actual address the issue . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the first line of the intro for the new book , Wilson-Raybould says : &amp;ldquo ; A central lesson instilled in me from a very young age was to be careful with words because you can not take them back &amp;mdash ; you must always speak the truth . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When you bring up the idea that , for many people , trusting the words of a politician has gone the way of straw boaters hats and stages covered in bunting , Wilson-Raybould pauses for a second and let&amp;rsquo ; s out a bit of a sigh . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It makes me kind of sad but I understand the reality we live in . This cynicism about politics and politicians is something that is hugely problematic for our democracy , &amp;rdquo ; said Wilson-Raybould. &amp;ldquo ; We need to change the culture or the nature of our politics to know that when we say something &amp;mdash ; that it has to mean something . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For me it comes from my culture being an oral culture . And if we don&amp;rsquo ; t speak the truth , the culture dies , &amp;rdquo ; added Wilson-Raybould. &amp;ldquo ; People should be more careful with what they say . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Speaking of what people are going to say , one has to wonder whether the Liberals will go after Wilson-Raybould in October or keep their heads down in hopes of keeping talk of SNC-Lavalin out of the campaign . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I can&amp;rsquo ; t put myself in the position of other candidates or parties , &amp;rdquo ; Wilson-Raybould said . &amp;ldquo ; I am hopeful that we can look at issues that are issues we all have to address like Indigenous issues and focus on them as opposed to focusing on individuals or trying to undermine the credibility of individuals . I&amp;rsquo ; ve faced that now for over eight months and I think that is very unfair and very debilitating and focusing energy where it shouldn&amp;rsquo ; t be . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even though eight months or so have passed since the country was glued to the SNC-Lavalin story , Wilson-Raybould reluctantly &amp;mdash ; she doesn&amp;rsquo ; t want to seem &amp;ldquo ; self-serving&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; admits she draws a lot of attention when she is out pounding the pavement in her riding . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Sometimes people just burst into tears . It&amp;rsquo ; s not about me . It&amp;rsquo ; s what I represented in that , if you believe in something , then you stick to it . You speak your truth without any fear of repercussions , &amp;rdquo ; said Wilson-Raybould. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However repercussions are a very real thing and politics is a dirty business . But rather than waiting and worrying about what may get lobbed her way , she is looking ahead and trying to be a good example to those who may follow her into the political arena . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I can inspire people to understand what my grandmother taught me , which was if you have a dream , if you are passionate about something and you work hard you can accomplish anything , &amp;rdquo ; said Wilson-Raybould. &amp;ldquo ; If there&amp;rsquo ; s a message to any little Indian girl in any corner of this great country or beyond , it is to know you can accomplish whatever you set your mind to if you work hard . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;75764;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;75791;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;75825;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; Dana GeeFri , 27 Sep 2019 18:18:22 **45;75856;TOOLONG ... announcement : Celine Dion Courage World Tour comes to Rogers Arenahttp : **35;75903;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;75940;TOOLONG ... title= " C ? line Dion performs in 2016 at Caesars Palace in Las Vegas . Email &lt;a href= " **28;78491;TOOLONG " **46;78521;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; David CarriggThu , 19 Sep 2019 16:45:41 **45;78569;TOOLONG ... NewsTelevisionElton John says Farewell Yellow Brick Road on final tourhttp : **36;78616;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;78654;TOOLONG ... title= " Sir Elton John will perform three shows at Rogers Arena this month as part of his Farewell Yellow Brick Road Tour . " alt= " Sir Elton John will perform three shows at Rogers Arena this month as part of his Farewell Yellow Brick Road Tour . " data-portal-copyright= " FERDY DAMMAN " **28;78690;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1091606 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Elton John Farewell Yellow Brick Road Tour&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;78720;TOOLONG : Sept. 21 to 24 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;78755;TOOLONG : Rogers Arena &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : &lt;a href= " https : **33;78791;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;78826;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; After 57 years performing , Sir Elton Hercules John ( CBE ) is saying goodbye to global fans . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Farewell Yellow Brick Road tour was announced on Jan. 24 , 2018 . With 300-plus dates , the piano-playing pop star&amp;rsquo ; s last road trip is scheduled to end in a two-night stand at The O2 in London in December . The musician born Reginald Kenneth Dwight on March 25 , 1947 , wants to spend more time with his husband David Furnish and their two sons , Zachary and Elijah . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He&amp;rsquo ; ll be home in time for Christmas . Still standing , but probably ready for a well-earned retirement . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Along the way , John makes multiple stops across Canada . It&amp;rsquo ; s testament to his popularity that none of these dates are single night shows . Vancouver actually has a three night-run . That&amp;rsquo ; s nearly 57,000 fans entertained at one stop and the other venues are of a similar size . Sir Elton is doing his best to not bow out breaking fan&amp;rsquo ; s hearts . The setlists on the tour are averaging 25 songs a night and almost all of those were hit singles , from the heights of his gaudy , glam-rock phase to the soundtrack successes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;78857;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;78893;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Such is the career of the artist who began making pocket change playing in pubs and local rock bands before first gaining attention in 1962 backing UK blues singer Long John Baldry in Bluesology . Changing his stage name to Elton John in 1972 , he and his lifelong lyricist Bernie Taupin , set about writing the first album . Titled Empty Sky ( 1969 ) , it didn&amp;rsquo ; t do much . The followup , 1970s Elton John , included the single You Song which went top 10 in both the U.K. and U.S. and remains in regular rotation on classic rock and adult contemporary radio to this very day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From there , it&amp;rsquo ; s a whirlwind of creative and commercial successes . By the time Honky Chateau became John&amp;rsquo ; s first U.S. No. 1 record in 1972 , his catalogue was already getting packed with the sort of singles most artists&amp;rsquo ; dream about achieving just once or twice . The John/Taupin songwriting machine made it look easy , and one year later , Goodbye Yellow Brick Road dropped including the title track , Bennie an the Jets ( U.S. No. 1 ) , Candle in the Wind , Saturday Night&amp;rsquo ; s Alright For Fighting and Funeral for a Friend/Love Lies Bleeding . And the songs just kept coming . By 2013 , Billboard hailed the artist as the most successful male solo artist on the trade magazine&amp;rsquo ; s Hot 100 Top All-Time Artists . He was just behind the Beatles and Madonna . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As fans prepare for the 72-year-old singer&amp;rsquo ; s arrival , here are five things to know about someone once referred to as &amp;ldquo ; Reggie . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;1 . The Top Tune&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Given the depth of the John/Taupin back catalogue , trying to get fans to agree on their top 10 tracks taken from the 30 albums the two have collaborated on is very difficult . Given there are over 58 Billboard top 40 hits included in the list over a 50 year period , there are generational shifts to take into account too . In a Sept. , 2018 piece in the Guardian , critic Dave Simpson compiled a top 50 songs list . Top spot went to Tiny Dancer , a six minute-long tune from Madman Across the Water which bombed upon release . Revitalized by its inclusion in director Cameron Crowe&amp;rsquo ; s film Almost Famous , the song has held the top spot in numerous lists published over the years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;78929;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;78965;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt;2 . The Voice&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sure , the wild costumes and accessories such as the US$5,000 spectacles that spelled his name in lights were great for gaining attention in the powder-happy hedonism of the glam rock era . Being a piano player in an era rife with showy guitar heroes was pretty different . But what really has been John&amp;rsquo ; s most potent tool throughout his career is his spectacular voice . He can croon , he can soar , he can scream and he almost lost it . In 1987 , John had surgery on his vocal cords to deal with a non-malignant lesion which resulted in his voice taking on whole new levels of deeper timbre and a noticeably rounder and fuller quality . He first showcased this on his 21st studio LP , Reg Strikes Back which included yet another Adult Contemporary chart No. 1 tune in I Don&amp;rsquo ; t Wan na Go on with You Like That . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;3 . The Osbourne Effect&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Bernie Taupin may be one of the only lyricists ever to wind up a household name , he isn&amp;rsquo ; t the only writer that John has turned to for words . Award-winning UK writer Gary Osbourne has been a frequent collaborator , coming up with Part Time Love , Little Jeannie and global hit Blue Eyes , amongst others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;4 . The Bitch is Back&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hands down , the best backstories of any John hit is the one for this snarky rocker from 1974 . As reported in the Guardian , the musician was very well-known for throwing mega-tantrums at the time . Following one such outburst , Bernie Taupin&amp;rsquo ; s ex-wife , Maxine Feibelman , exclaimed &amp;ldquo ; Uh-oh , the bitch is back . &amp;rdquo ; Taupin loved it and turned it into the lyric for this celebrated rocker , which featured John&amp;rsquo ; s good friend John Lennon on tambourine . John has joked that it is &amp;ldquo ; my theme song . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;5 . Retirement ; not Retirement&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Easily one of the most prolific artists to come out of the classic rock era , Sir Elton has been busy in the soundtrack scene since the 1970s , hitting a career high with The Lion King in 1994 . And , yes , Gnomeo &amp;amp ; Juliet could be a career low as far as these projects go . OK , the biopic Rocketman didn&amp;rsquo ; t perform as hoped either , but it&amp;rsquo ; s all so much water off of John&amp;rsquo ; s Donald Duck costume&amp;rsquo ; s back . He has inked a deal with Universal Music Group to represent both his five decade-long catalogue as well as new music . There will doubtless be new music . Who knows , maybe there could be a tour with Taron Egerton playing John performing only music from his 1970s records . The music is certain to have a life of its own after Elton hangs up his concert platforms . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;79001;TOOLONG " **36;79032;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;79070;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;79099;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynMon , 16 Sep 2019 18:05:00 **45;79130;TOOLONG ... " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;79645;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1092501 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;BREATH AND LIFE : &lt;/strong&gt; At the Vancouver Playhouse recently , Philip Lyall and Nimisha Mukerji screened , 65RedRoses , their 2009 film about since-deceased cystic fibrosis patient Eva Markvoort . The fundraising event promoted CF awareness and organ donation . Although the lauded movie wasn&amp;rsquo ; t an Oscar contender , attendees Alison Snowden and David Fine won one for their animated short , Bob&amp;rsquo ; s Birthday , and earned three other Oscar nominations . Like Markvoort , Snowden received donated lungs , but survived . After a virus destroyed her own , Snowden was put into an induced coma for a month and deemed to be too weak for transplant surgery . Business and personal partner Fine said &amp;ldquo ; a breakthrough idea&amp;rdquo ; entailed awakening her and rebuilding strength during non-stop treatment by ECMO ( extracorporeal membrane oxygenation ) heart-lung-bypass technology . It worked . Donated lungs arrived , Dr. John Yee undertook the surgery , and Fine and the recovering Snowden completed another Oscar-nominated short , Animal Behaviour . Snowden&amp;rsquo ; s proposed acceptance speech at the February , 2019 Academy Awards ceremony would have praised VGH , her surgical team and Canadian medicine generally . However , the award went to Toronto director-writer Domee Shi&amp;rsquo ; s Bao. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;79675;TOOLONG ... title= " The Sequoia Quartet 's Catherine Teng , Kai Chow , David Han and Davin Mar demonstrated the prowess of VSO School of Music students . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;79777;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1092494 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;FOOTBALL FAME : &lt;/strong&gt; B.C. After retiring in 2018 , Potenza-born Buono will be inducted into the Italian Cultural Centre&amp;rsquo ; s Hall of Fame Oct. 4 and possibly called &amp;ldquo ; the pride of all B.C.&amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;DOWN PARRYSCOPE : &lt;/strong&gt; As we consider electing more parliamentarians with no more authority than pets on a leash , a Scottish high court judge has ruled that parliament&amp;rsquo ; s role in scrutinizing the government is a central pillar of the UK&amp;rsquo ; s constitution , which follows naturally from the principles of democracy and the rule of law . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;79807;TOOLONG " **34;79836;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 604-929-8456 &lt;/p&gt; Malcolm ParryFri , 13 Sep 2019 20:31:34 **45;79872;TOOLONG RacingBC **29;79919;TOOLONG &amp;amp ; BeautyFootballLocal ArtsLocal HealthMusicReal EstateWomenScott Thompson 's Buddy Cole Monologues addresses psychic fallout of AIDS erahttp : **37;79950;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;79989;TOOLONG ... title= " Scott Thompson as Buddy Cole . " alt= " Scott Thompson as Buddy Cole . " data-portal-copyright= " Picasa " **28;80025;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1087074 " /&gt; &lt;h2&gt;Scott Thompson 's Apres le De'luge&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Buddy Cole Monologues&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;80055;TOOLONG : Sept. 19 to 21 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;80090;TOOLONG : The Cultch Historic Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : From $26 , at &lt;a href= " https : **33;80126;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;80161;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; The character Charles Budderick &amp;ldquo ; Buddy&amp;rdquo ; Cole first turned up on screen in the groundbreaking CBC series The Kids in the Hall . The jaded , sharp-tongued , openly gay role created by Scott Thompson became one of the show&amp;rsquo ; s most celebrated characters during its 1989 to 1995 run . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From romps with Queen Elizabeth ( also played by Thompson ) and other rich and famous friends to increasingly outrageous adventures , Cole&amp;rsquo ; s monologues were unlike anything else on TV. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As Buddy declared in a classic sketch : &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m an actor , singer , dancer , model , Canadian . I can make the word Canadian sound sexy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After the series , the character continued to make appearances , including recurring segments on The Colbert Report reporting from the Sochi Olympics and other locales . Thirty years on , Thompson is dusting off the Ascots and lam&amp;eacute ; smoking jackets and bringing the Buddy Cole Monologues to theatres across Canada in a show titled Apres le De'luge. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thompson , 60 , says it&amp;rsquo ; s particularly appropriate being Buddy now because he always envisioned the character to be around his age . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When I first started doing Buddy , it was a very different world where homophobia was so prevalent and so obvious and encouraged by society , &amp;rdquo ; said Thompson . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Comedies normally had a moment where the male characters had to prove they weren&amp;rsquo ; t gay and show revulsion about it for laughs &amp;mdash ; such as Steve Martin and John Candy waking up together in Planes , Trains and Automobiles &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; and it&amp;rsquo ; s still happening . So there was this enormous hostility towards Buddy from the mainstream but also from the gay community , because he represents a kind of mirror . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;80190;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;80232;TOOLONG ... more about Vancouver **28;80267;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;80297;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;80333;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;80364;TOOLONG ... North : The latest from the B.C. TV and film **29;80400;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Modelled after a real life &amp;ldquo ; brief date , &amp;rdquo ; Buddy was bound to be controversial . The character is crass , effeminate , relentlessly critical , and full of opinions that certainly wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t pass muster at most social gatherings . Yes , Buddy is a stereotype . But Thompson says the character is also genuine , and that rubbed a lot of people the wrong way back in the day &amp;mdash ; and in the present . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Being Buddy , behaving like that , talking like that , dressing like that was , in many ways , already a joke , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; A guy being like that was pretty hilarious , and you could laugh at him from a point of superiority . For all of the advances we have made against homophobia , the effeminate , older gay male is still pretty low down in the pecking order , and I made sure that Buddy reflected that awareness and threw it right back . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A fine example of this would be the Cole monologue from his gay bar called Buddy&amp;rsquo ; s , which the character says he paid for with money saved from not smoking . Addressing the breaking stories of the day about outing Hollywood stars , Buddy observes that he really hopes that Liberace isn&amp;rsquo ; t gay because he doesn&amp;rsquo ; t want him to be , and also name drops former Donald Trump and Rupert Murdoch legal counsel Roy Cohn . Disbarred for unethical conduct in 1986 , Cohn had been involved in outing homosexuals during the McCarthy era , costing people their jobs and more during the &amp;ldquo ; Lavender Scare . &amp;rdquo ; He died of AIDS on Aug. 2 , 1986. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Liberace and Roy Cohn &amp;mdash ; it&amp;rsquo ; s like being told Satan&amp;rsquo ; s a fag , &amp;rdquo ; says Buddy . &amp;ldquo ; Maybe that&amp;rsquo ; s why God is such a homophobe and Satan&amp;rsquo ; s so sexy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By the end of the 5-min piece , Cole has taken on gay-bashing comics like Andrew Dice Clay , declared himself the new Lenny Bruce , and announced that Buddy&amp;rsquo ; s will have no standup comedy : &amp;ldquo ; just family entertainment for the Queer Nation . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thompson says lines like that led to people being antagonistic when he first began to play the character &amp;mdash ; and they remain so today , but for different reasons . From the political committee groupthink of the &amp;rsquo ; 80s to the &amp;ldquo ; modern puritanism&amp;rdquo ; of the present day , the world just doesn&amp;rsquo ; t seem a place to embrace Buddy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Now , young people are so conditioned to be angry at the character behaving like that because it&amp;rsquo ; s apparently insulting , as if being effeminate is wrong in itself , &amp;rdquo ; says Thompson . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There is an enormous amount of delusion and denial going on with people , and Buddy&amp;rsquo ; s just not part of that . Plus he&amp;rsquo ; s very sexual , and that was never something that was allowed and there has been a kind of subconscious reaction to forget what that meant in the AIDS years . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;80431;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;80473;TOOLONG ... more about Vancouver **28;80508;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;80538;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;80574;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;80605;TOOLONG ... North : The latest from the B.C. TV and film **29;80641;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; This last point has been addressed in numerous programs lately , particularly in a powerful scene in the 2019 Netflix Tales of the City miniseries . At a dinner party , &amp;nbsp ; generational conflict flares up between a group of older men and a younger one over the fact that the impact of the AIDS epidemic doesn&amp;rsquo ; t seem to resonate with him . It&amp;rsquo ; s powerful stuff , and Thompson says that the scene brought him to tears . Buddy was born of that era. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Back then , it was such a terrible time , that there was no guarantee that I&amp;rsquo ; d make it , it was quite likely I would get it , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I practised safe sex very early on , but for the first few years after I came out no one knew . People have no memory of how it affected people , and I look around at gay men in my age group and we are so broken . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;80672;TOOLONG ... title= " Scott Thompson as Buddy Cole . " alt= " Scott Thompson as Buddy Cole . " data-portal-copyright= " Picasa " **28;80708;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1091252 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thompson says that he&amp;rsquo ; s waited 30 years to deal with the psychic fallout of the era , which is where the Apres le De'luge title comes from . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Likening his generation to &amp;ldquo ; Vietnam vets that no one wants to look at or acknowledge , &amp;rdquo ; he says he thinks a lot of the contemporary gay community wants the AIDS years forgotten because it somehow &amp;ldquo ; reflects badly . &amp;rdquo ; Among those who Thompson knew who didn&amp;rsquo ; t make it was the man that Buddy was modelled upon . He says that , in an ironic way , he&amp;rsquo ; s keeping him alive carrying on the character . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He was a very flamboyant , very sexual , powerful , effeminate gay man and I was so full of self-loathing at the time over my own non-classically masculine behaviour , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It meant a lot to me , even though it was a really fleeting moment in time . You reflect on those sorts of things as time goes by . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; None of the 11 monologues featured in the show have ever been seen on TV , as they were all penned after The Kids in the Hall ended , and cover the period from 1995 to the present day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The idea is that as everything around him is changing , Buddy doesn&amp;rsquo ; t because he&amp;rsquo ; s perfect , &amp;rdquo ; Thompson said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He&amp;rsquo ; s like a rock in the middle of a river , it just has to flow around it . A kind of poignancy builds as you go on the journey , because he&amp;rsquo ; s not afraid at getting to the truth , even if it makes him look bad . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thompson says that Buddy gets away with saying things that he never would in his own standup act . Why ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Because Buddy Cole just happens to be some weird kind of perfect delivery system . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div class= " page " title= " Page 1 " &gt; &lt;div class= " layoutArea " &gt; &lt;div class= " column " &gt; &lt;p&gt;Thompson is appearing as part of the &lt;a href= " https : //www.pitheatre.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Pi Provocateurs 2019-20 Presentation Series&lt;/a&gt; , which showcases " fearless local and national artists " in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;80738;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;80774;TOOLONG &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;80810;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;80852;TOOLONG ... more about Vancouver **28;80887;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;80917;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Then , as what the Dutch might call tit voor tat , Nederlands Dans Theater snagged Regina-born Ballet B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s artistic director , Emily Molnar , to lead its 27- and 18-dancer companies . Former Ballet B.C. dancer Molnar has steered the once-moribund company through a decade of break-even-or-better seasons to critical acclaim here and on national and international tours . Addressing dancers , staff , board members and supporters recently , she said : &amp;ldquo ; What we have done together is remarkable . &amp;rdquo ; Then , to rueful smiles all around , &amp;ldquo ; It doesn&amp;rsquo ; t happen easily . &amp;rdquo ; Encouragingly , though , dancers &amp;ldquo ; now have more opportunities to stay at home with full-time or almost full-time work . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MORE GLOBALISM : &lt;/strong&gt; Finland native Kari Turunen has succeeded Vancouver Chamber Choir&amp;rsquo ; s Illinois-born founder and 47-year artistic director , Jon Washburn . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;81436;TOOLONG ... title= " Thomas and Amy Fung 's annual garden party and singalong drew corporate , cultural and political guests as well as UBC and SFU 's presidents .
