" data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;82000;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1088154 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;DOWN PARRYSCOPE : &lt;/strong&gt; Late French president Charles de Gaulle , whose vetoes made petitioning Britons wait 12 years to join what is now the European Union , might relish their current opera bouffe to get out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;82030;TOOLONG " **34;82059;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 604-929-8456 &lt;/p&gt; Malcolm ParrySat , 07 Sep 2019 00:38:31 **45;82095;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; ChildFashion &amp;amp ; BeautyFestivalsLocal ArtsLocal BusinessLocal **42;82142;TOOLONG the next generation for employment in Vancouver 's entertainment industrieshttp : **30;82186;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;82218;TOOLONG ... title= " Christopher Bennett , the executive producer and head of marketing at the Vancouver Film School . Photo : Arlen Redekop/Postmedia " alt= " Christopher Bennett , the executive producer and head of marketing at the Vancouver Film School . Photo : Arlen Redekop/Postmedia " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;82254;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1079301 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Declaring to your parents that you want to work in film and TV production or gaming design isn&amp;rsquo ; t something you say under your breath anymore . From crews to directors and screenwriters , programs are in place to produce some of the most talented employees in these growing global industries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The demand for new content to populate streaming services is generating more entertainment industry jobs than ever in an already bustling business . The demand for workers with a wide-ranging skill set is stronger than ever . But is &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; the next generation of workers in Hollywood North feeding an American machine with the stories it wants at the expense of telling Canadian stories ? &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s definitely a period of changing models right now as the streamers don&amp;rsquo ; t do production deals in the same way broadcasters or major studios did , and the whole idea of what rights and global licensing is are changing the traditional models , &amp;rdquo ; said Creative B.C. CEO Prem Gil. &amp;ldquo ; The service industry is a big , strong part of the story in British Columbia , as is the talent . Because you have to remember that all of those shows &amp;mdash ; U.S. , Canadian , wherever they are from &amp;mdash ; are being touched by B.C. talent all along the way . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver has the training infrastructure in place to put Canadians in key positions that would have likely been filled by foreigners in the past . Animation Career Review rated the Vancouver Film School the No. 1 Global Animation School and the No. 1 Canadian Animation School this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On a tour of the downtown campus with VFS executive producer and head of marketing Christopher Ian Bennett , the scope of training being offered to the next generation of Hollywood North employees is clear . Student productions are shooting on sound stages , including a room kitted out to resemble a &amp;ldquo ; Beautiful B.C.&amp;rdquo ; landscape complete with trees , shrubs and a solid rock face to climb . In another room are carefully detailed set models . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;82284;TOOLONG ... title= " A landscape sound stage inside the Vancouver Film School . Photo : Arlen Redekop/Postmedia " alt= " A landscape sound stage inside the Vancouver Film School . Photo : Arlen Redekop/Postmedia " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;82320;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1079146 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Hit the lights and see how much work goes into these models , &amp;rdquo ; said Bennett . &amp;ldquo ; You use a light source to study how it plays in the room to determine film shots . What happens when a car drives up , when someone walks outside ? It&amp;rsquo ; s better to figure it out here than when you are rolling on a crewed-up set . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The facility is preparing workers for the local and international market , said VFS managing director Jon Bell . Students come from Canada , Mexico , Korea , China , India and other locales to learn a wide range of industry-related skills . Film is still the most in-demand program , but the bottom line is different . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In terms of industry demands , the global video game industry is three times the size of film and television combined , &amp;rdquo ; said Bell . &amp;ldquo ; The skill set and employability in visual effects is one of the best , and Vancouver has one of the biggest industries in the world now . We have revised programs to reflect the industry . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Local crews operating in every capacity of production are regularly rated some of the best in the business . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our talent is in global demand and there is certainly a desired trickle-down effect that comes back to domestic productions fully owned and created here , &amp;rdquo ; said Gil. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Last year , the provincial government enhanced the film incentive B.C. tax credit to include writers , which hadn&amp;rsquo ; t existed before . As that happened , Creative B.C. , Netflix and the Producers Association were able to create the new Pacific Screenwriters Program focused on building writing rooms here and supporting our writing talent to be able to work on both their own content and in service writing rooms . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;82350;TOOLONG ... title= " Christopher Bennett , the executive producer and head of marketing at the Vancouver Film School . Photo : Arlen Redekop/Postmedia " alt= " Christopher Bennett , the executive producer and head of marketing at the Vancouver Film School . Photo : Arlen Redekop/Postmedia " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;82386;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1085777 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As Kim&amp;rsquo ; s Convenience and Schitt&amp;rsquo ; s Creek &amp;mdash ; both CBC programs &amp;mdash ; recently proved , there are global audiences for good stories with universal appeal . Raila Gutman at the Pacific Screenwriting Program says generating local intellectual property ( IP ) is often overshadowed by service bureau work . Writers wanting to develop their own projects usually have to go to Los Angeles or Toronto to get things moving . The program aims to change that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re training writers , not the emerging ones but mid-level and senior ones too , to develop their own IP in B.C. , &amp;rdquo ; said Gutman. &amp;ldquo ; Historically , there wasn&amp;rsquo ; t a workforce here to give the industry the impression we could have writers&amp;rsquo ; rooms based here , so they set them up in L.A. Last year , we hosted a summit where we invited Netflix , CBC , Canadian and American broadcasters and major studios out to B.C. to get them engaged with the writing community . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the same time that the industry is being introduced , writers are being taught the ins and outs of working in a writers&amp;rsquo ; room . Gutman says the program aims to provide the tools required to work in writers&amp;rsquo ; rooms . Proof of its success will be seen in an increase in locally developed and produced intellectual property . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kendrie Upton , the executive director at the Directors Guild of Canada , B.C. District Council , says the same situation of underutilized talent has applied in the past to locals helming productions . Programs such as the Directors Initiative provide vital training . The DGC B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s Director Showcase ( Sept. 6 to 8 at the Vancity Theatre and Playhouse ) showcases B.C. directed work in film and TV to provide exposure and advocate to get more Canadians in the director&amp;rsquo ; s chair . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not that it&amp;rsquo ; s not happening , Upton explains , shouting out to companies such as Nomadic Productions and programs such as Netflix&amp;rsquo ; s Travellers who employed local talent . It&amp;rsquo ; s just that the DGC B.C. recognizes challenges in getting its members noticed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our market share has traditionally clung around the 30 per cent mark , &amp;rdquo ; said Upton , a 30 year industry veteran . &amp;ldquo ; That would be not be good enough anyways , but with production so much higher now it hasn&amp;rsquo ; t increased with it . We did some market research recently where we reached out to our L.A. clients to say we&amp;rsquo ; ve got some really talented B.C. directors you could be using on your locally shot shows and the standard return was &amp;lsquo ; we don&amp;rsquo ; t know who they are . ' &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;82416;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver 's first film commissioner David Shepheard with executive director of the Vancouver Film &amp; Media Centre , Nancy Mott . Photo : Nick Procaylo/Postmedia " alt= " Vancouver 's first film commissioner David Shepheard with executive director of the Vancouver Film &amp; Media Centre , Nancy Mott . Photo : Nick Procaylo/Postmedia " data-portal-copyright= " NICK PROCAYLO " **28;82452;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1085784 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Perhaps the art of the pitch should be added to industry training manuals . Done right , you get results . Vancouver film commissioner David Shepheard notes that a recent tour of Hollywood North by various members of the U.K. entertainment industries produced exciting results . From industry organizations to government bodies , establishing relationships is key to driving local IP and employment . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; A lot of good business relationships were established out of us bringing over people on that tour , including a financing deal to establish a new range of kid&amp;rsquo ; s animated films using the Bear Grylls brand between Platinum Films and Burnaby-based Bron Studios animation wing , &amp;rdquo ; said Shepheard. &amp;ldquo ; This kind of content creation reflects the changing market where the globe becomes increasingly smaller and content reaches markets that wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t have been reached in the past . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver born-and-raised Shawn Williamson started Brightlight Pictures in 2001 . In its 18 year-long history , the company has cultivated an impressive combination of local intellectual property and service work ranging from TV&amp;rsquo ; s The Good Doctor to Disney&amp;rsquo ; s hit Descendants properties . He echoes the sentiment on the importance of building relationships to recognize the talent base in the local industry . And he supports the proposition that the combination of service work and local intellectual property is key to keeping the industry vital and providing continuing opportunities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have multiple examples of how we were able to leverage relationships developed from service work we&amp;rsquo ; ve done on shows we didn&amp;rsquo ; t own into creative work on ones we did , &amp;rdquo ; said Williamson . &amp;ldquo ; An example is an international film we did a few years ago called Horns , starring Daniel Radcliffe and directed by a French director named Alex Aja , which lead to a developing a project called the 9th Life of Louis Drax , developed within the Canadian content system . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; One of the joys with the bulk of production of shows being produced here and internationally with the demand for new content is that those who were otherwise having challenges breaking down the doors of Hollywood are being given a shot and we have a deep talent pool . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Williamson says that they are presently crewing up something in the realm of a &amp;ldquo ; thousand technicians , actors , background people , writers and so forth&amp;rdquo ; on their shows . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Phil Klapwyk , the business representative at IATSE local 891 knows all about those jobs . While producers , writers , directors and actors may get the glory , but it&amp;rsquo ; s often the hard-working crews that make the movie getting more work . His local represents 19 departments with 135 job titles that all work in film and television production careers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For every one director that lands a job , there are usually about 120 talented technicians and artists working behind them to make their vision come to fruition , and a lot of these are people are those with correlative skills in the real world , from electricians to carpenters to make up artists . &amp;rdquo ; said Klapwyk. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There is a labour market information project due to be released by the government very soon that suggests a five-per cent attrition rate throughout the industry per year up against a three to four per cent annual growth which means we need at least an eight to nine per cent workforce increase to keep up with that pace . For my 9,000 members , that means 720 jobs a year . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hollywood North&amp;rsquo ; s future looks bright , although not without challenges keeping pace with predicted growth . Expect to see more and more outreach and educational efforts to entice young people to consider careers in the existing creative industries as well as those to come . As Christopher Hobbs of Vancouver custom software development firm TTT Studios notes , the city is well-positioned for new businesses in emerging entertainment technologies . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Because of its proven past in film , TV , gaming and digital entertainment and the infrastructure around it , there are continuing great opportunities , &amp;rdquo ; said Hobbs . &amp;ldquo ; VR ( virtual reality ) and AR ( augmented reality ) are natural fits in terms of tech development , and we can become a global leader there beyond the entertainment infrastructure . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;82482;TOOLONG " **36;82513;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;82551;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;82580;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;82611;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? ArtsLocal **34;82754;TOOLONG of Hollywood North 's busy entertainment industryhttp : **32;82790;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;82824;TOOLONG ... title= " Pixomondo producer Matthew Welford in action at the company 's Gastown studio in Vancouver , BC. , September 4 , 2019. " alt= " Pixomondo producer Matthew Welford in action at the company 's Gastown studio in Vancouver , BC. , September 4 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " NICK PROCAYLO " **28;82860;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1087887 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hollywood North is a $3.58-billion dollar industry , employing thousands in a variety of capacities ranging from actors and producers to special effects artists and support personnel . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The mechanics of the industry are changing rapidly , due in part to the amount of work being generated by streaming services such as Netflix and Amazon in addition to the traditional networks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And visual effects ( VFX ) and animation are at the heart of it all , as there are very few productions today that don&amp;rsquo ; t have VFX and/or animation involved to some degree . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To get a feel for the state of the industry today , we talked to three people about their personal history , the current projects and the future of Hollywood North . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;82890;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver actress Jennifer Cheon Garcia appeared in season 5 of Supergirl in a guest role . " alt= " Vancouver actress Jennifer Cheon Garcia appeared in season 5 of Supergirl in a guest role . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;82926;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1085287 " /&gt; **31;82956;TOOLONG a **32;82989;TOOLONG **46;83023;TOOLONG ... front of the **32;83071;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Jennifer Cheon Garcia transitioned into acting from modelling and dancing right out of high school , getting in on the ground floor working as an extra . And it was scary . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Nobody knew then it was going to get like this with so many productions going here , everyone wanted to get that 01 visa ( for the U.S. ) and get to Los Angeles and make it , &amp;rdquo ; said Cheon Garcia . &amp;ldquo ; If you came home , you failed , it was harsh . But in 2013 , living in L.A. , I looked at everything that was available to me and realized that I was auditioning for roles that were in Vancouver and I thought &amp;ldquo ; bigger fish/smaller pond , hopefully . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yes , there were naysayers . But being able to pursue her goals and live at home was a dream come true . The reality was that some part of almost everything she found work in was happening here . And if the need arose , the geographic location meant a few hours on a plane to get to Tinseltown. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For sure , you can do it all in one day if you&amp;rsquo ; ve got the stamina &amp;mdash ; and people do it constantly , &amp;rdquo ; said Cheon Garcia . &amp;ldquo ; Everyone wants content now , and I believe that they want content that speaks to people with well-told stories that they can relate to no matter where they are . It&amp;rsquo ; s not so much about being from Canada or one specific spot &amp;mdash ; although for sure there is strong Vancouver pride &amp;mdash ; as it is in reflecting this diverse world we live in. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;83105;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;83140;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Cheon Garcia currently&amp;nbsp ; plays Ivory , the leader of an all-female vampire faction called the Sisterhood in the Vancouver-shot SyFy supernatural action series , Van Helsing. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Season four of the fantasy horror series about the adventures of Vanessa Van Helsing , the future descendant of monster hunter Abraham Van Helsing , in a world ruled by vampires airs on Netflix in Canada on Sept. 27. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of Mexican/Korean heritage , Cheon Garcia landed her first role at age 19 in director Julia Kwan&amp;rsquo ; s acclaimed feature Eve and the Fire Horse and won a 2016 Best Actress Leo Award for her work in the acclaimed web series The Drive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;With her martial arts expertise , Cheon Garcia has an in-demand skill set . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Sci-Fi has always been a pretty natural home for me , because it was easier to cast a six foot-tall Mexican/Korean girl there than in more traditional stories , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s what I loved about the Drive and about Canadian content like Kim&amp;rsquo ; s Convenience , because we are used to seeing someone like that in any role here . I still love demons and vampires though , because who wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t want to get dressed up and spend 14 hours a day sword fighting and high-kicking ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Looking forward , Cheon Garcia is excited about getting behind the camera and pursuing her goal to get more involved in the hands-on side of production , making sure that the diversity of our world is reflected up on screen . It 's a transition that many in the local industry pursue as wearing more hats means more varied work . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;83176;TOOLONG ... title= " Jonathan Walker is Vancouver-based actor and producer . Photo : Kevin Clark " alt= " Jonathan Walker is Vancouver-based actor and producer . Photo : Kevin Clark " data-portal-copyright= " Kevin Clark " **28;83212;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1085266 " /&gt; **43;83242;TOOLONG &g; ... your skill set to suit industry **31;83287;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Industry veteran Jonathan Walker is a man of many talents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The producer of season four of Van Helsing and Netflix&amp;rsquo ; s Wu Assassins , starring Indonesian action hero Iko Uwais ( The Raid ) , Walker has acted in the X-Files , The Killing and now the coming TNT series adaptation of Korean director Bong Joon-Ho&amp;rsquo ; s action thriller Snowpiercer. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Working multiple angles is great , but Walker says it can get a bit overwhelming as well . For a portion of last fall , he was doing 12 hour days on set of Wu Assassins as a producer and &amp;ldquo ; then driving across town to do the same in front of the camera on Snowpiercer as an actor . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Any demands for his character to look wiped out and worn were achieved without putting too much mental focus into the method . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;83320;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;83356;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; The very fact that this kind of multiple jobs scenario exists is proof of the booming local business , said Walker . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are obviously in a golden era of television production , with all of the major film production centres across the country seeing record numbers , budget spends and those sort of things , &amp;rdquo ; said Walker . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; From the perspective of crew members , equipment providers , ancillary services like hotels and caterers and actors , we are all very , very happy . It&amp;rsquo ; s a win for everybody when you get great projects like Van Helsing , which get in front of a lot of eyeballs because that keeps them going . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We used to be a location that companies came to purely because it was cheap and American money went a lot farther paying crews , cast , etc. , &amp;rdquo ; said Walker . &amp;ldquo ; Science fiction shows tend to be shot on tighter budgets and there were a number of those shows &amp;mdash ; X-Files , Andromeda , Stargate SG-1 &amp;mdash ; that were shot here 15 years ago for that reason . What happened was that we got enough of those productions that we got very good at making them and industry noticed and then higher budget ones turned up and now we&amp;rsquo ; ve hit the zenith where Altered Carbon had a first season budget of $800,000 to $1 million an episode . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; **31;83392;TOOLONG happens without VFX **33;83425;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Matthew Welford came to the West Coast looking for work in the entertainment industry 10 years ago and never left . He&amp;rsquo ; s not alone in that regard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Welford has a masters degree in digital special effects from the National Center for Computer Animation in Bournemouth , England . He is currently employed as the VFX supervisor at Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Pixomondo , an international special effects studio founded in 2001 in Germany by CEO and executive producer Thilo Kuther . It now has offices in Los Angeles , Beijing , Shanghai , Toronto , Frankfurt , Stuttgart , Montreal and Vancouver . The studio has been involved in everything from Mary Poppins Returns and the Child&amp;rsquo ; s Play reboot to Game of Thrones and Star Trek : Discovery . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Vancouver has cemented itself as a hub with both deep resources , technology and talent , &amp;rdquo ; said Welford . &amp;ldquo ; Which is a very different impression than it had when I got here 10 years ago on a work visa . There was already a good base then , but a lot of crew were brought in globally . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And a lot of us stayed , and wanted to stay , and every week I&amp;rsquo ; m seeing another VFX artist getting their citizenship because they want to be working in a proven , mature industry . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The combination of a few busy major facilities and high demand for jobs keeps senior level workers employed even through the cyclical ups and downs , which means the talent base runs deep . Pixomondo Vancouver started with a two person office after being relocated from Los Angeles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are about 120 people now , going from about 45 people last year , &amp;rdquo ; said Welford . &amp;ldquo ; That meant taking two top floors of the offices we were in and over-doubling our capacity in a short time to reflect the demands of the workload . Being next door to Los Angeles , which is still the de facto centre of where the studios are , even ones such as Lion&amp;rsquo ; s Gate that started in Canada , has really proven advantageous to the growth of the industry . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;83460;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;83495;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; It seems that , no matter how long these actors , producers , supervisors and more have been working in Hollywood North , they all say the industry was &amp;ldquo ; already there&amp;rdquo ; when they started . The change now is that the depth of talent is being put to work like never before and there is no indication of it slowing down either . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Part three in our three-part VFX series : Facing the Future prepared . Is telling our stories still important in a global industry undergoing seismic changes ? And how is the industry preparing the next generation of Canadians for work in Hollywood North ? &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;83531;TOOLONG " **36;83562;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;83600;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;83629;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynThu , 05 Sep 2019 17:47:29 **45;83660;TOOLONG ... **33;83707;TOOLONG Cuba sharpens his award-winning songwriting even more on Sublimehttp : **33;83742;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;83777;TOOLONG ... title= " Alex Cuba . Latin Grammy Award-winning musician in promo shot for album Sublime release . 2019 PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Alex Cuba . Latin Grammy Award-winning musician in promo shot for album Sublime release . 2019 PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " Robin Pedraja " **28;83813;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1084042 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Alex Cuba&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;83843;TOOLONG : Wednesday , Nov. 6 , 7:30 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;83878;TOOLONG : Kay Meek Arts Centre , 1700 Mathers Ave. , West Van &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;83914;TOOLONG : &lt;a href= " https : **30;83952;TOOLONG ? ut ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **25;83984;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Seven albums in , Alex Cuba has upped his game again with Sublime . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To appreciate that statement , you should consider the Smithers-based singer/songwriter has already racked up four Latin Grammy Awards and two Grammy nominations for the Best Latin Pop Album . No surprise the legendary Cuban vocalist Omara Portuondo ( Buena Vista Social Club ) and rising star Silvana Estrada make appearances on the John ( Beetle ) Bailey-engineered record . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; They are the only other performers on the album recorded in Canada , Mexico , Cuba and Spain . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alex handles every other instrumental duty on the disc and he&amp;rsquo ; s never sounded better . As he readies for an extensive round of touring across North America and elsewhere , the musician discussed the realities of breaking into markets where his music is more readily consumed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As an almost entirely Spanish language artist , his fame is far greater outside of this country than in it . He&amp;rsquo ; s fine with that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Mexico has emerged as quite the place for me in the past few years , &amp;rdquo ; said Cuba . &amp;ldquo ; According to streams on Spotify and other sites , it is the place that listens to me the most and we are , obviously , paying attention to that . It&amp;rsquo ; s the kind of place you can find any kind of music really and where they are quite open to a lot of variety in the music . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s a good fit for the artist whose work can best be described as pop/rock . Owing to his understanding of Latin American genres ranging from bolero and bachata to rancheros to son , Cuba has always worked elements of these forms into his hook-laden material . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But there are considerable influences drawn from classic pop tunesmiths like the Beatles or funk masters like Stevie Wonder turning up in gems such as his hit Directo . On Sublime , he opens with a complex rhythmic romp titled Yo No S&amp;eacute ; that could have been on an early Brazilian Tropicalismo release by Caetano Veloso or Gilberto Gil. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The artist loves the comparison , noting he still listens to a lot of recordings from that era because they are &amp;ldquo ; so real , so honest , the music is pure and it keeps it sounding alive . &amp;rdquo ; That he can write something which hearkens back to that creative hubbub is impressive . That the opening song on Sublime was an instant recording is amazing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Yo No S&amp;eacute ; was the last song on the album , written on the spot on the last day in the studio , which came from a bass line I was jamming and loved and knew I had to use , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So I was writing the lyrics while I was playing all the different parts , shouting them out and we were both rolling on the floor by the end of it blown away by the results . Essentially , the concept of the song became was Alex Cuba rehearsing with Alex Cuba , so I made this video for it that has me , in colour , and a bunch of black and white Alex Cubas playing the song . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;84011;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;84047;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; The track sets the vibe for Sublime and it&amp;rsquo ; s spot on for the collection of a dozen songs ranging from the tender lead single Voz De Corazones to the easy flowing duet Solo M&amp;iacute ; a with Mexican star Leonel Garcia . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dominican star Alex Ferreira and Cuban musicians Kelvis Ochoa and Pablo Milan&amp;eacute ; s also appear on the album . For those in the know , this lineup along with Portuondo and Estrada demonstrates the kind of pull that Cuba has in the upper levels of Spanish language stars . People want to perform his songs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I grew up listening to people like Pablo Milan&amp;eacute ; s , who is the Cuban Bob Dylan , and Omara and others and really wrote the songs that they appear on with their spirit in mind , because their work really shaped my writing , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And when I decided to go to the next level and see if they would honour me by appearing , I was really taken about that they knew who I was and knew my work and said yes . Canada may not be the best place for putting my music forward , but the reason that my music is fresh and different is because it comes from Canada . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sublime holds a special spot in Cuba&amp;rsquo ; s heart as it is the record , he believes , that took him back home to his birth country of Cuba and reflects the journey he&amp;rsquo ; s taken to where he is today . The musician originally hails from Artemesia , Cuba , and both he and his brother , Adonis , were schooled in music by their guitar-playing music teacher father from a young age . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alex and Adonis settled in Victoria in 1989 after Alex married a Canadian he met in Cuba . The Puentes Brothers duo released a Juno-nominated debut , eventually deciding on solo careers around the early 2000s . Alex moved to his wife&amp;rsquo ; s hometown of Smithers in 2003 and has run his career from the central B.C. town since . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He says he can make music anywhere and finds the natural beauty and chill vibe completely suited to crafting his , frankly , more tropical material . He is constantly jotting down ideas , melodies and more on his phone wherever he is and that process was used on Sublime . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I recorded this album in Gibsons with Garth Richardson , because Beetle Bailey said he had a great sounding room there , &amp;rdquo ; said Cuba . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So I packed up all my toys &amp;mdash ; seven guitars , congas , a standup bass , bongos , shakers , triangles and more &amp;mdash ; into my car and drove down . It took two days and I had my computer connected to the stereo and the phone on voice memos. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I had a lot of time to myself to think about things like &amp;ldquo ; oh , we need a handclap there and this would be a good place for more backing vocals. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The ultimate goal is to give each song the right vibe and Cuba feels Sublime has it in large amounts . All the technological tricks in the world can fake it . If it&amp;rsquo ; s there , you can make it happen anywhere . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;84083;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;84114;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **26;84152;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;84180;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s2 " &gt;&lt;a href= " **27;84211;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK **35;84240;TOOLONG class= " s1 " &gt; to report a typo. &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Is there more to this story ? ArtsMusicThe National takes its sound to the cinema on I Am Easy To Findhttp : **36;84413;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;84451;TOOLONG ... title= " The National performs at Deer Lake Park on August 28. " alt= " The National performs at Deer Lake Park on August 28. " data-portal-copyright= " Graham MacIndoe " **28;84487;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1076150 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The National&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;84517;TOOLONG : Aug. 28 , 5:30 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;84552;TOOLONG : Festival Lawn at Deer Lake Park , Burnaby &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and inf&lt;/strong&gt; o : $65 at &lt;a href= " https : //www.livenation.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **28;84588;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; I Am Easy to Find is the title of the eighth album by the critically acclaimed and commercially successful American indie rock band The National . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A short 27-minute film of the same name directed by indie feature director and graphic designer Mike Mills , starring Academy Award-winning actor Alicia Vikander , arrived with the record . Using rearrangements of songs from the album as the soundtrack , the black-and-white work tells a woman&amp;rsquo ; s story from childhood to passing , touching on those moments in a life that define it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Anyone familiar with the band&amp;rsquo ; s sound and , particularly , singer Matt Berninger&amp;rsquo ; s imagistic and emotional lyricism , won&amp;rsquo ; t find it surprising that they are making movies . The group&amp;rsquo ; s music is immediately cinematic and increasingly artsy . Its expansive orchestrations and Berninger&amp;rsquo ; s rounded baritone provide the soundtrack to the film , which includes subtitles . It&amp;rsquo ; s a beautifully moving work from a band with two decades of documenting human frailty and subdued rage in increasingly complex albums. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I Am Easy to Find takes all the group&amp;rsquo ; s past achievements and ups the ante considerably . Where past records put Berninger&amp;rsquo ; s voice at the centre of everything , he is matched equally by a different featured female vocalist on most of the songs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The cast includes heavyweights such as Ireland&amp;rsquo ; s Lisa Hannigan , French folky Mina Tindle ( Pauline de Lassus ) , This Is the Kit&amp;rsquo ; s Kate Stables , Sharon Van Etten and the Brooklyn Youth Chorus on the show-stopping Dust Swirls in Strange Light . One of the standout performances in an album loaded with them is longtime David Bowie bassist and backing singer Gail Ann Dorsey , who slays on the opening track You Had Your Soul With You . It begins what winds up being something of a 16-song conversation that comes to you from some very abstract starting points . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bassist , multi-instrumentalist Scott Devendorf said the members haven&amp;rsquo ; t forgotten how to write straight-ahead songs or being able to define them . He vehemently disagrees with any suggestion that the more experimental side projects he , Berninger , guitarist Aaron Dessner , guitarist Bryce David Dessner and drummer Bryan Devendorf are involved in have bled into The National&amp;rsquo ; s music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;84618;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;84654;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re pretty aware of what a straight-ahead song is . We write a lot of them , &amp;rdquo ; said Devendorf. &amp;ldquo ; Songs with words being pushed along with choruses , verses , poetry and structure or a piano ballad or two . But this time , working with Mike Mills on the album , he was very encouraging about us working outside of our comfort levels , and that&amp;rsquo ; s where we went . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No , this in no way means that I Am Easy to Find is a &amp;ldquo ; difficult&amp;rdquo ; record or bound to be filed further back in the future listening pile than something like 2010&amp;rsquo ; s breakout High Violet . It&amp;rsquo ; s the sound of a band going where they wanted with confidence and hitting the marks . Plus , for all its lush orchestration , it&amp;rsquo ; s not so complicated that it can&amp;rsquo ; t be delivered live . Developing the new tour strategy did involve some &amp;ldquo ; market research . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Starting back in April , we did five preview shows where we would show the film , have a short Q&amp;amp ; A and then play as much of the record as we could in the time frame , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; We only did it from start to end in New York because that was when we had the Brooklyn Youth Chorus , who don&amp;rsquo ; t tour . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From there , the band worked out ideas for the coming tour and Devendorf said it&amp;rsquo ; s been great . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Yes , the songs were constructed in such a way as to be playable , but are very vocal , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Fortunately , our friends who joined us in the recording have been able to join us for the additional instrumentation . And we have been able to have two or three of our friends who recorded with us &amp;mdash ; Lisa Hannigan , Pauline De Lassus , Kate Stable , Gail Ann Dorsey &amp;mdash ; present for one or more of the shows and scheduling so that everyone is always overlapping . It&amp;rsquo ; s mainly been Kate and Pauline , but everyone has their own projects going too , so it needs to be flexible . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The bassist admits it would be impossible to present the music on the new album without at least one additional singer and additional musicians . But that has been the case going back a few albums now , and The National can certainly work with it . The band also manages to keep making music and touring with two pairs of siblings in the same band . One need only look back at The Kinks , Oasis and others to know that family can sometimes be the worst combination for group longevity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;84690;TOOLONG ... title= " The National play Deer Lake Park in Burnaby on Aug. 28. " alt= " The National play Deer Lake Park in Burnaby on Aug. 28. " data-portal-copyright= " Graham MacIndoe " **28;84726;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1080216 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Do the many side projects &amp;mdash ; from Bryce Dessner&amp;rsquo ; s classical composing to Berninger&amp;rsquo ; s EL VY , Aaron Dessner&amp;rsquo ; s Big Red Machine , Bryan Devendorf&amp;rsquo ; s Pfarmers and the brothers Devendorf&amp;rsquo ; s LNZNDRF &amp;mdash ; help keep the peace and keep everyone feeling fresh ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Definitely , the influence of all of those side projects is always there when we get together , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; We will all be finding new things through those experiments and bringing them into the room when we are working again , and it keeps it fresh and interesting and launches new ideas . Even if some of them don&amp;rsquo ; t work out , sometimes the exercise can be really important and liberating , too . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As to whether the Dessners or the Devendorfs are the Gallagher Brothers in the band , Scott laughs heartily . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Oh , I really have to say neither . No one fights at that level like those guys , not even close . We can&amp;rsquo ; t live up to that level , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m actually fascinated by the Gallaghers after watching Supersonic . I never really followed them when they were happening aside from knowing some of those big hits , but after watching the show I had better understanding of why they are always in the news . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Perhaps its because Oasis is all swagger and Mancunian aggression , while The National is all shuffle and Midwestern apologism . This is a band that gets itself pumped up to go on stage blasting Johnny Mathis&amp;rsquo ; s A Great Night for Crying as they walk on . Clearly , this is not a band aiming for arena chants and sing-alongs. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve been alternating between that and a Richard Reed Perry song from one of his Quiet River of Dust albums , &amp;rdquo ; said Devendorf. &amp;ldquo ; I never remember the title of the song , but it&amp;rsquo ; s been really good matched with the Mathis , which was Matt&amp;rsquo ; s find . Both of those kind of cleanse the palate of any other contemporary music before we embark on what we&amp;rsquo ; re going to do , and it&amp;rsquo ; s a pretty funny message to send too . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Aaron is the &amp;ldquo ; set list master&amp;rdquo ; when the band hits the road . Devendorf says that with this many albums behind them , The National finds it needs to take in people&amp;rsquo ; s expectations of &amp;ldquo ; hearing the hits , if we really have any . &amp;rdquo ; But not really being a singles band at all , he said they just decided to put together about five different takes on the set list and &amp;ldquo ; put in those songs that people really want to hear , but just didn&amp;rsquo ; t know that they did . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As to whether The National Netflix series is coming after I Am Easy To Find , there are no plans . After the long tour winds down , he and brother Bryan will hit the studio for another LNZNDRF recording and all the other members will be working on various things too . While that happens , new music for The National&amp;rsquo ; s next album will naturally emerge . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;84756;TOOLONG " **36;84787;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;84825;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;84854;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynThu , 22 Aug 2019 19:18:24 **45;84885;TOOLONG ...
